# FIELD FORGE — CONSTITUTIVE VISION HOME
## The historically reproduced, actor-independent social relations and differentiated possibilities that a developing person needs

## R13 — operative first consultation: the survey-internal social dialectic (2026-11-25)

**Current user direction:** make the following function prominent in the actual shared development spaces, not just conserved in historical theory. MULCH THE LEAST OF THESE selects the survey-internal differentiated mass-intersubjective pressure field, comparatively neglected by the recent native-material/world-gate advances. It is a constitutive FIELD FORGE function even without the priority pack. MULCH YOUR NEIGHBOR makes its upstream and downstream contracts visible; HULCH makes its concepts revisable. No new inventory, universal carrier, obligatory geometry or independent mandate to divert P0. Read §§V–IX, XI–XII and XVI below in conjunction with this front; [capability HOME](01_FIELD_FORGE_EXISTENT_CODE_AND_CAPABILITY_HOME.md) and [G4 receiving proposal](EXTERNAL_STRUCTURAL_EVIDENCE/03_RECEIVING_AND_NEXT_DECISION.md#r13--operative-cross-owner-receiving-proposal) give the reuse and authority boundaries.

### The positive object: relative ideological positionality in a social dialectic

The unit of interest is not the average opinion of a demographic group. It is the differentiated distribution of genuine same-respondent response complexes (including incompatible-looking principles/applications, rival moralities, theological/political vocabularies, minority and counterconfigurations) across independently described social positions and their changing relation to other formations. The empirical response complex may be internally contradictory; its position-conditioned spread may itself be contradictory. Discover rather than prescribe these complexes. A contradiction in meaning is a semantic hypothesis about the response acts, not a numeric correlation alone; a contradiction of material interests requires separately supported or explicitly modeled interests. A repertoire's association with an advantaged position is already a meaningful descriptive result, without a causal theory or external pack. A tendency can be widespread among materially subordinate groups yet disproportionately concentrated in an advantaged position. Neither private assent nor group prevalence is institutional force.

### Construct the two independent views and their correspondence

- **Position view C:** source-observed resources/income, wealth where measured, education/credential, occupation, work status, organizational responsibility, age, sex/gender, locality/urbanity, technology access/use, group membership and other pertinent coordinates. Treat these as independently measured candidate position roles, not universal power scores. Freeze actual source-specific meanings and ancestry for each contrast; income is not ownership, credential is not command, technology use is not power. Consider intersection, nonlinearity, contradictory resource profiles, locally specific advantage, and change of ranking by arena. A locally advantaged combination need not be globally dominant. Keep explicitly measured authority/decision-making positions distinct from proxies and from externally qualified command. Do not choose an advantage orientation after looking at the disposition it is supposed to explain.
- **Disposition view D:** complete supported joint response patterns, not a flattened left/right index or a person. Preserve semantic response-act distinctions, alternatives, exceptions and rare positives. A candidate complex may be nested, overlapping, split, negated or redefined by HULCH; preserve the exact underlying source cells under concept revision. Position and disposition active measurement ancestry must be disjoint, including derived features; alternate role builds if justified, never discover homology by using the same answers twice.
- **Correspondence:** on one source-coadministered risk and admissible estimand, compare P(D|C), P(C|D), P(D,C), P(D), P(C). The conditional on position asks prevalence within that position; the reverse asks who composes the repertoire; joint mass asks population/sample reach; the baseline guards against base-rate illusions. For a supported exact motif d and two positions c1,c2 retain both counts and denominators, conditional difference, uncertainty or explicit unsupported status. Enrichment P(d|c)/P(d) may be informative only with positive baseline; it is not power. Compare conditional reversals and interaction under multiple independently measured coordinates, not one-variable main effects. Expose both within-position diversity and between-position association. Exact zero, unknown, silence, not coadministered and not searched differ. Existing RAW full-case candidate returns sample support only; no unearned weighted population estimate. Multiple universes remain separate unless qualified correspondence permits comparison.
- **Find the contradictions:** upstream/HULCH proposes concepts or rival motifs from actual co-travel, asks the next discriminating joint/reverse query, and revises on the result. FF asks how the motif is distributed, what it traverses, which independently positioned contrasts matter, whether its apparent unity fractures, and which rival complex occupies or crosses the same positions. Compare resource-advantaged alignment and penetration into dependent positions independently. Do not label a statistical minority counterhegemonic or declare a position's material interest from a demographic proxy. Preserve observed contradiction, semantic interpretation, and material hypothesis as separately addressable.
- **Hegemonic/subhegemonic evidence:** preserve the seven E117 axes in IX-A: prevalence, cross-position reach, high-position alignment, independently grounded political-power alignment, geography/centrality, historical persistence/expansion, countercomplexes. The survey-internal axes are positive deliverables without an independent external issuer. Political power, institutional reproduction, public enactment, recognition, devaluation and conversion remain separately qualified dimensions. No obligatory total score, no universal geometry; direct joint/conditional/reverse operations first, historical Core B only for genuine shape/neighborhood questions that direct queries cannot answer.

### One concrete construction court (not a new engine)

Start from the already located WVS W7 Q33/Q34/Q279 T06 question (scarce jobs gender/national priority and reported employment). The two normative items do not measure hiring conduct, the employment response does not measure scarcity or ownership, and split routing must survive. Next, use actual admitted source metadata to identify additional independent candidate position measures (education, sex, age, resource status, locality, occupational authority if present and coadministered); do not invent item IDs or force all measures into a single joint. Ask for joint position–repertoire and baseline from one saved current I03 query where lawful, then use S04 projections for multiple selected intersections, reverse composition, minority alternatives, conditional reversal and rival concept. If a needed axis is not coadministered, return the actual limitation and choose a supported narrower question, not multiply margins. On sourcefree data use adversarial fixtures with identical repertoire margins but opposite advantage alignment, same conditional prevalence with different composition due to base rates, different intersection behavior despite similar single-axis margins, and a minority rival within an advantaged position. Demonstrate selected first reader changes its **information** when source joint changes, without requiring a changed world event or person. Historical A/C and contemporary S04/FF01 supply the comparison capital; optional B only on demonstrated consumer/discovery need. R11 already implements two selected states of one conditioner and proposal-selected reverse inquiry; multiple-position/complex repertoire service and actual native issuance are not demonstrated by that slice. Do not reimplement I03, estimators, I05 scheduling or whole FFX by name. Use G4/PP26-11,13–19,21,24,25 and exact source to select the smallest residual.

### What this produces for other authorities

PYAT/F1–F4 serves source-qualified answer configurations, forward/reverse joint and observation support; DHIKRYMULCH/HULCH proposes and tests semantic motifs and contradictory interpretations, with empirical counterevidence able to revise concepts; FF constructs independent position/disposition comparisons and relative social-field evidence. External source pack enriches and challenges these with native material and institutional relations, never acts as a compulsory input to the descriptive survey-internal operation. Post-FORGE/SEAM world owners use qualified alternatives to constitute particular institutions, public practices, material arrangements and eligible Other offers; CO and NEIGHBOR realize independently persisting Others, actual access, enactment, confrontation and appropriation, not survey-derived assigned psyche. Parent entrance preserves whole-survey respondent-coherent alternatives; Nadia encounters actual historically differentiated surroundings. Feedback from actual world/actor construction identifies new FF questions, not new source facts. See downstream HOME first consultation. The candidate's stronger claim authority and actual first native receiver remain held; no protected adoption.

No conclusive final allocation of every legacy V550+ function, stage, engineering requirement or under-theorized mechanism is yet established. **This section does not establish a second PROGRAMMA or update the canonical statuses.** Its controlling prior sources are [N028's original constitution and jurisdiction](https://github.com/maidiu/rawlsing/blob/25830fe7462bb9e29bc4087e6ae14f3f84cec377/data/ddm_build_copy/recovery_integration/semantic_relation_R1_through_Job3/R1_20260825/PRE_MAIN_RECONCILIATION_R1/PROJECT_INHERITANCE_R1/CAMPAIGNS/N028_FIELD_FORGE/CORPUS/ORIGINAL/ad335b22bcd6__01_RAWLSING_CONSTITUTION_AND_JURISDICTION.md), [Global-HQ's adopted September R2](https://github.com/maidiu/rawlsing/blob/096b419ed8fd3d07c87cb74bcc4890c2f1a8ec54/data/ddm_build_copy/recovery_integration/semantic_relation_R1_through_Job3/R1_20260825/PRE_MAIN_RECONCILIATION_R1/GLOBAL_HQ_FIELD_FORGE_R2_TERMINAL_ACCEPTANCE_R1/00_GLOBAL_HQ_ACCEPTANCE_RETURN.md), and the existing [G4 S01–S11 guide](https://github.com/maidiu/rawlsing/blob/integration/pre-main-reconciliation-r1/PYAT_CONTROL_ROOM/PROGRAMMA_PYAT/05_ROADMAP/README.md). Actual current code/callers and original competent authority outrank any stage name in an old DDM document.

### The distinction is between five questions, not two lists

For **each** legacy V550+ operation, source idea, problem, and newly desired capability, the correct disposition must answer:

1. **Constitutive need** — what information, transformation, explanatory or operational ability does the real final person/world system need, *and under which named consumer request*? Is the problem experimentally identifiable or still under-ideated?
2. **Earliest lawful producer** — which existing upstream source/measurement/query/learned/structural issuer can actually produce the input without inventing respondents, observations, weights, causal rights or institutional power? Does such an issuer even exist?
3. **Differentiating FIELD FORGE transformation** — if lawful inputs already exist, what *further actor-independent social-relational fact or query* must FF itself produce that no existing upstream function already does? A direct S04 result may already satisfy a requested answer. A nonbinding concept requires no new FF code.
4. **Post-FORGE first reader / realization** — which exact F6/F7/V553–V558/N9/N10/PRE-V3/SEAM/SOROK caller *uses* the information to produce its own independently authorized changed world option, opportunity, interaction, event, actor experience or state? Where is the actual first read, not a receipt that merely echoes a source hash?
5. **Disposition versus actual estate** — compare **best historical articulation**, **independent correction to its omissions**, **present implementation** (protected/candidate/historical/none at immutable HEAD), **present planned/G4 owner**, **executed positive and hostile proof**, **scientific/actor/source authority**, **unresolved architecture/engineering**, and **smallest action that could change a selected recipient's answer**. The output may be REUSE, CONNECT, EXTEND, REPAIR, conditional RESEARCH, OWNER-HELD, NO-BUILD, or RETIRE-WITH-FUNCTION-CONSERVED. These are local review decisions, **not new official G4 status enums**.

### Architectural omissions that should *not* simply become FF tickets

- **Upstream deficit:** if C11 cannot economically reopen same respondent k-way queries, or original35 source/weight/risk/method is not authorized, the missing operation belongs upstream. FF may specify the *needed input*, but must not create a third survey or source authority.
- **Producer/consumer wiring deficit:** if V552-like structural identity already exists but is dropped, aliased to a permission flag or never applied in M4/N9, repair the original producer/bridge/first-reader contract. A prettier FF claim carrier is not the remedy.
- **Institutional issuer deficit:** if no competent real employer, property, land, care, command or geopolitical source/crosswalk can issue the claim, that is an **explicit missing issuer/architectural assignment problem**, not an FF permission to infer domination from a survey.
- **FF-native relational deficit:** where real upstream evidence and a named reader exist but the available simple empirical answer *cannot* express the necessary actor-independent relation, comparative pressure, ambiguity or counterposition, then and **only then** test a claim-specific FF capability against existing historic/current operations.
- **Post-FF realization deficit:** where a source-scoped relation has no changed selected N9 option or N10W response, the first world reader or interface may be missing—even if FF already produces a correct neutral precursor.
- **Actor/psychology deficit:** where SOROK lacks an actual encounter→recruitment→action→reconstitution circuit, the remedy is downstream software construction; a society-wide respondent matrix cannot author an individual.
- **Under-ideated scientific/architectural question:** if neither the historic corpus nor current G4 resolves what *kind of computation* could distinguish explanatory mechanisms or validate power relations, record the exact question and likely competent authority as **UNRESOLVED**. Do **not** invent missing FF modules or falsely claim the existing vision is perfect.

**Consequence for the broader requested G1–G5/PO PYAT LIVE final map:** the six-file Field Forge recovery is **a bounded source and responsibility input**, not the overall final implementation plan. Before any revised execution agenda, take the same function-grain court across **all upstream producers, FF, conditional F6/F7, world publication and actor consumers**, then reconcile current protected/candidate code, tests, G4 and actual first-reader starvation. A historical design can be incomplete; the final map must allow genuine new **requirements**, but allocate each only after demonstrating which incumbent cannot satisfy it and which competent owner may build/issue it.

## ORIGIN PRIORITY — FIELD FORGE WAS NOT DESIGNED IN 2025

**User-controlled historiographic priority, 2026-11-23:** The direct reconstruction begins at **May 2026**, not with a 2025 typology model. Field Forge arises from the *limitations and achievements of actual DDM structural/evidence machinery* and the subsequent problem of how a social world should be represented for downstream person formation. Track this specific causal sequence, not an ancestor parade:

1. **May 2026 V540–V558 / V549b:** the original **v2 attitude-cloud pack has now been recovered** from the user-supplied ZIP (D098), including its specific rule that V549 branch co-signatures and V549b answer-anchored clouds are distinct and non-demographic. Historical code fragments are present (D103). The **pack's exact May 11 creation date remains unverified**: the separate May 11 conversation itself was not recovered. Do not conflate the 2026-11-23 ZIP packaging timestamp with composition.
2. **June 25 V552/V552J/V553:** field information can be genuinely computed at V552 and still fail to reach temporal-pressure consumers because structural identifiers are dropped, permission metadata substitutes for meaning, zero-row handoffs pass vacuous tests, and demo keys masquerade as structural pressure. The four separate June original assessments give specific generated-output counts and exact defect classes. Those historical failures **motivate the kind of source/relation/consumer discipline** that Field Forge later required; they are not automatically defects in today's code.
3. **June 20 original conversation, June 26 architecture, then August V5/APP:** D100 is a directly inspected self-dated **June 20** first-person conversation (underlying session archive not independently authenticated), explicitly instructing that strata be **responsive to the focal position and observed social contexts, not prefilled boxes**. D101/D102 preserve its contemporary boundary: the structural compiler need not search for every ideology or author anyone's felt identification. The July 6 handoff remains a **separate, unverified DATE/CONTENT LEAD**, not this June 20 transcript. August V5/APP further develop these requirements.
4. **August 29–31 original N028 Field Forge:** position/capital space, disposition/attitude space and claim-conditioned correspondence/pressure topology become explicit candidates to replace a fixed nine-strata model. Retain N028 originals E020–E032 and their anti-circularity/observability/estimand criteria, but do not pretend this was already an accepted universal three-space engine.
5. **September 2 R2 adoption:** after downstream first-reader and A/B/C ablation, the *adopted* solution is the producer-owned, typed capability/relation, exact negative and claim-specific geometry regime—not a compulsory master field object. Current code G4 and exact source/tests determine what exists.
6. **Downstream V553–V558 / N9 / N10 / SOROK:** the field is necessary only insofar as it makes empirically/structurally qualified **other-position social possibility and material conditions operable** for these consumers; it does not own their world events, actor choices or acquired psyches.

**Research weighting:** May–August immediate formation **PRIMARY**; September R2 and current code/first-consumer truth **CONTROLLING**; later SOROK requirements **TERMINAL NECESSITY**; pre-May 2026 only **TARGETED LOST-IDEA CHECK**. This distinction is about relevance and authority, not about denying the early records exist.

### Recovery from the original supplied ZIP changes what is known, not who owns the machine

**Archive custody:** `Rawlsing_located_20261123.zip`, SHA-256 `fef58bd94913202ffd040faa89bdc5da46ed8b2696a4422112dc253adb0a5169`, 112 substantive archived files in four directories. Source ledger D098–D106 contains exact member paths and individual member SHA-256. These are **uploaded original documents and historical code**, not yet committed protected source. Do not fabricate GitHub blob URLs for them.

The critical original exchange is D100's `ORIGINAL_CONVERSATION_nine_strata_2026-06-20.txt`: at the transcript's **2026-06-20** date the user independently insisted that every stratum can relate to others through changing identification, alienation and pressure; that demographic/context position should select which *actual evidentiary* strata matter; and that V552–V557 should compute **structural priors rather than search for every hypothetical settler theology or precompute the actual person's unconscious stance**. Its United States/California/church and Iraq/Sunni/Shia descriptions are **illustrative questions**, not observed sociological source facts. The actual actor's inner magnetization, defensive organization and later criticality belong to downstream development, not a Field Forge causal coefficient.

**The original v2 V549b lock matters precisely:** its law says `V549` branch co-signatures and `V549b` answer-anchored non-demographic opinion co-clouds are distinct sibling products. A person/group can give the same answer from a different social position and a different surrounding co-response repertoire. The original v2 contract says no imaginary cross-survey joint, no demography masquerading as attitude, no codebook-only measurements, no individual belief assignment. The historical D103 Python implements some true respondent-ID pair joining and source-local Cramér's V/branch comparisons, **not** general k-way survey design; it also writes manually invented 'confidence' and raw n copied into 'weighted' columns, and the supplied CSV has **zero data rows**. These are authentic historic code issues, not current G4 failures.

This direct-source evidence strengthens September R2's **claim-specific, producer-owned relations** but does not retrofit the old nine strata into universal objects or make “ruling-class opinion” measurable from high income alone. A source-observed higher-SES group may be a legitimate descriptive comparator. Identifying actual ruling power, resource control or ideological hegemony requires separately competent structural/empirical authority. Any actual person, social contact, subjective relationship to a church, employer threat or acquired inner contradiction requires a later particularized world/actor reader.

## I. The single fundamental problem Field Forge must actually solve

**The world that conditions a future person is neither a list of demographic attributes nor an already completed personality.** It consists of objective and social positions, distributed material resources, institutions, enforceable permissions and sanctions, shared practices, measured orientations, competing coherent attitude configurations, historical situations and evolving material conflicts. Our available surveys show fragments of people's positions and dispositions at historically specific moments. They rarely directly record the full material control relations or an individual's lifetime.

If downstream SOROK is to generate a parent and, eventually, Nadia, **what does the observed and explicitly hypothesized social world make possible, likely, available, expensive, risky, attractive, blocked or contradictory, for which kinds of socially situated positions—and under what qualification of evidence?**

The terminal questions, repeatedly expressed in G4, are:

1. **What is possible?** Distinguish real source-observed/structurally admitted/hypothetical/unknown alternatives, not just modal outcomes.
2. **What is facilitated?** Measure/qualify context-specific opportunity, support, conditional co-travel and material enabling relations—without making a predicted attitude into a person's desire.
3. **What is inhibited, repressed or prohibited?** Differentiate statistical scarcity, an institutionally enforceable prohibition, an economic cost and a person's later internal inhibition.
4. **By which forces or powers?** Use actual actors/institutions/ownership/authorization/mediating structures when separately sourced; do not infer power from prevalence or identity labels.
5. **In contradiction with what other tendencies?** Preserve empirical counterpositions, minority co-travel, conflicts among material requirements, and nonadditive context effects without a single moral or class scalar.
6. **Under what changes of conditions might these relations shift?** Field Forge answers the *counterposed evidence/conditioned relations*; downstream N9/SOROK executes the actual counterfactual world change and historical activity.

**The constitutive unit is a source- and claim-qualified answer or typed relation**, reconstructable from its competent producer, not a mandatory one-size-fits-all population object, graph or static manifold. A future person is a first consumer of those conditions, **not** their statistical realization.

### I-A. The positive relation is not an attitude label and not an encyclopaedia

The original [fixed-source correction E107](https://github.com/maidiu/rawlsing/blob/3065c22ab03be56796399d266856cbc7fa22f933/data/ddm_build_copy/recovery_integration/semantic_relation_R1_through_Job3/R1_20260825/FIELD_FORGE_R1/00A_FIXED_SOURCE_CORRECTION_AND_CURRENT_MISSION.md#L13-L109) is more than an anti-drift footnote. FIELD FORGE exists to maximize the world that can truthfully be made available **from the existing source universe**. The old control-room invitation to obtain PISA, HBSC, ICCS, TIMSS/PIRLS or youth longitudinal data was explicitly revoked for that campaign. A theoretically necessary distinction that cannot be measured is an ontology constraint or a typed observability limit, not an invented procurement obligation. This constraint applies at R1 campaign grain; it does not revoke any separately competent current source authorization.

The field is not a single map. It is a set of real answerable *relations of differentiated possibility*: between positions and configurations of expressed orientations; between a configuration and the conditions under which it appears; between social settings in which a resource or practice has different value; between a repertoire and the reach, standing or power alignment of its social carriers. The empirical and material grounds are distinct even when a downstream world question needs both. It is not necessary to compute the ultimate causal explanation before a valid observed redistribution can help construct a plausible adult. Equally, a future world must be more than such redistributions: where actual institutional capacity is required, source and meaning must come from its own competent material owner.

The functional demand therefore has two sides that must not cancel one another: **preserve the richest supported distinctions and do not fabricate unobserved relations**. [E109](https://github.com/maidiu/rawlsing/blob/3065c22ab03be56796399d266856cbc7fa22f933/data/ddm_build_copy/recovery_integration/semantic_relation_R1_through_Job3/R1_20260825/FIELD_FORGE_R1/02_THEORY_TO_ENGINEERING_CONSTRAINT.md) translates every candidate theory into observable relation, lawful operation, governed semantic interpretation and unsupported residue. The latter is a positive intellectual result, not a zero and not an excuse to treat all material life as noise. This is why September R2's weakest-lawful-representation law is not a mandate for the weakest imaginable social description.

### I-B. The actual object of sufficiency: an inhabited world that can form a person

User correction 2026-11-24 (preserved verbatim in [R5 §0](EXTERNAL_STRUCTURAL_EVIDENCE/04_INHABITED_SOCIAL_FIELD_CONNECTION_ARCHITECTURE.md)): the purpose is persona formation within inhabited historical/material conditions, habituses, overlapping interpressuring strata and hegemony; distinguish objective-objective material constraint from mass-intersubjectively negotiated groups-amongst-groups-within-groups. This changes the optimization target, not the requirement for truthful source semantics.

Three mutually constituting orders must remain distinct: (i) bodily/material and independently operative resource/control/constraint relations; (ii) mass-intersubjectively reproduced meanings, classifications, practices, recognition, public expectations and institutional forces; (iii) actor-specific acquired psychological organization. The first two are independent of the particular focal actor, not independent of social activity. Neither is reducible to the other or to what the actor consciously believes. Actual participation can reproduce or transform both. FF serves relational intelligence about the first two; the world's and psyche's becoming remains downstream. A material relation may affect a child without ever becoming a consciously named explanation; a circulating explanation may be false and still formative.

The sufficiency criterion is therefore not the completeness of a global economy description but whether relevant material/public/social relations and their transformations are present for a possible life. Represent remote power through consequential relations, including future dependencies and public meanings; actual named ownership/coercion is required when historically pertinent or claimed, not for every person. No additional broad global dataset is selected. Preserve PP26-16's stronger actual-world requirement and its evidence ceiling, rather than falsely declaring it solved or holding all fictional construction. A coherent explicit fictional arrangement can become operative under the proper owner without claiming it was historically observed.

This neither diminishes empirical PYAT correspondence nor allows arbitrary cultural stereotypes: original source evidence constrains and differentiates possible repertoires and contexts; generative world construction particularizes what no source could have measured about a fictional family. Scientific claims, model choices, realized world facts, and acquired meanings carry different truth conditions.


## II. What exists, before any projected fuller machine

The separate code HOME is authoritative for this draft's *source-static* code account. These are the non-negotiable distinctions to retain directly in the vision.

| Proven/located implementation | What is already calculated or applied | What this does NOT yet demonstrate |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Protected I03/I05/recursive** | Genuine bounded respondent-linked multi-target query, scoped candidate discovery and recursive evidence/negative paths | Original35 native all-wave viability or sociological meaning by default |
| **Protected GT12 FF→D→M1/V554→M4** | Fixed Q86 conditional count profiles, typed source/claim admission, actually resolved CONTEXT relation | Generic all-source F5, objective material power, arbitrary social mechanism |
| **#1247 generic FF01/S04 code** | Fully resolved selected-risk k-way RAW joint, exact conditional/reverse-context counts, minority-cell candidates and unranked tables on that same joint | Protected receiving; positive partial-risk joint, multiselect/repeated response, >16,384 Cartesian cells, per-cell respondent membership, weighted inference, causal score or physical effect |
| **Historical FFX A/B/C** | Exact configurations, conditional redistribution, local categorical position/disposition geometry, distances, MDS, neighborhoods, stress, jackknife stability, integrated A/B/C canaries | Automatic modern native, semantic science or all-world physical adoption |
| **Protected N6 materializer/J04 publication** | Exact *pre-authorized* objective position, permission/time/replay fact and bounded V557/V558 CONTEXT resolution | A producer of genuine property/asset-control and labor-reproduction relations |
| **Protected sourcefree M4/M5/N9/P41/R05/R112** | Actual test-domain field values and applied eligibility/fee/exit/civil costs, matched empirical and structural selected-world reads | General lived work/care institution and person formation |
| **#1173 C4/C5/C6** | Source-coherent candidate adult contextual cases and research branches, typed nonissuing world handoff | SOROK first person draw, parent-history continuity, actor state changes |

**No proposed function may enter this HOME as `MISSING` until it has been compared with an actual existing producer, positive/negative code behavior, and a named reader.** A code module can be real and narrow. A theoretic insight can be profound and still not belong to its source author as runtime owner.

## III. The *constitution*: minimum R2 grammar that every richer vision must obey

[The adopted R2 act](https://github.com/maidiu/rawlsing/blob/096b419ed8fd3d07c87cb74bcc4890c2f1a8ec54/data/ddm_build_copy/recovery_integration/semantic_relation_R1_through_Job3/R1_20260825/PRE_MAIN_RECONCILIATION_R1/GLOBAL_HQ_FIELD_FORGE_R2_TERMINAL_ACCEPTANCE_R1/00_GLOBAL_HQ_ACCEPTANCE_RETURN.md) and [protected capsule](https://github.com/maidiu/rawlsing/blob/827714e83743056939894a39736d72ea16bdc6dd/data/ddm_build_copy/recovery_integration/semantic_relation_R1_through_Job3/R1_20260825/PRE_MAIN_RECONCILIATION_R1/PROJECT_INHERITANCE_R1/FIELD_FORGE_R2_ADOPTED_AUTHORITY_R1/01_ADOPTED_AUTHORITY_CAPSULE.md) require:

1. **Producer-owned exact information** remains accessible by lawful dereference or materialization; Field Forge never copies raw data to claim the source's authority as its own.
2. **An explicit typed actor-independent claim/relation or query identity** is declared, with exact source/measurement/unit/scope/risk set, applicable permission and negative state.
3. **Immutable provenance closure and deterministic replay** bind any relation to what was actually observed; a source query can remain authoritative without a newly stored universal FF carrier.
4. **Representation follows the claim.** Identity, order, distance, topology, embedding, conditional probability and structural mechanism demand different evidence. More coordinates are not automatically more truth.
5. **Negative evidence is primary data.** Observed zero, not exposed, nonapplicable, unresolved, refused, unsupported, incompatible, and no certified causal meaning are not one interchangeable FALSE.
6. **Cross-context transport is a separate operation.** `Local_A + Local_B + authorized typed map + comparator + falsifiers`, or `NO_TRANSPORT`; no fixed world coordinate system.
7. **Strong sociological names require a separately competent issuer.** FIELD, FIELD_SPECIFIC_CAPITAL, HEGEMONY/SUBHEGEMONY, HOMOLOGY, CONVERSION, PRESSURE_FOOTPRINT beyond neutral association, PRESSURE_PATHWAY and STRUCTURAL_MECHANISM remain `BLOCKED_EXPLICIT_AUTHORITY` where such authority is absent. The social phenomenon is not declared unreal.
8. **Absolute actor boundary.** A source-population relation can condition a fictive actor's possibilities but never itself issue beliefs, memories, motive, childhood facts, resistance or an acquired psychic organization.
9. **Physical implementation is semantically subordinate.** Query/virtual, persistent, index, reduced sufficient statistic or hybrid carriers are legal alternatives; require changed downstream value and resource measurements before selecting one.

These constraints are not a bureaucratic tax on the vision. They prevent the model from manufacturing a world in which the scientific label becomes truer than the people and institutions it purportedly describes.

## IV. The empirical observatory: preserve exactly what respondents genuinely share

### IV.1. Source capability is not respondent exposure

The original N028 source stress pass ([E031](00_VISION_ARCHITECTURE_AND_PREIMPLEMENTATION_SOURCE_LEDGER.md), Arab Barometer VIII example) states these distinctions. Its historical authored grade was FILE_LOCATED; the prior source-passage pass directly reopened substantive sections (`READ_SECTIONS_20261123`), so the older locator-only statement no longer controls. The recovered E119 original case-selection contract independently requires unequal observability to remain visible. Neither is a native source execution or population science grant:

- Is measurement X in this source/wave/country/form at all?
- Was respondent i **eligible** and **shown** X under routing, random split and prior answer constraints?
- Was X answered; how many answers were permitted; what is the source-native value?
- Can the same respondent legitimately possess X and Y (and Z) for this claim?
- Is nonresponse an observed refusal, don't-know, user-missing, not asked, structurally nonapplicable, or authority unresolved?
- Does the source authorize using this sample/weight/design for this *particular estimand*?

The Arab Barometer VIII questionnaire mixes randomized section order, split forms, country versions, select-all, answer-piped follow-ups and branch-exposure predicates. **A data column being present does not make it a measure for every record.**

An FF observable joint is not all columns with matching names; it is a source-qualified intersection of actual respondent exposure and valid *shared* risk sets. Its representational topology can be disconnected: no amount of imputation automatically makes absent coadministration real.

### IV.2. Complete coherent repertoires precede typology

A genuinely coadministered, fully resolved k-way observed respondent configuration may combine apparent ideological opposites: social welfare support, electoral abstention, low state trust and obligations to religious kin. The empirical object is the *entire observed combination and its conditional support*, not a generic "collectivist", "religious", "alienated" or "Type 6" latent property.

Field Forge should be able to answer, without factorizing away correlations:

- What configurations of Y1…Yk are actually measured together and observed in context C?
- Which conditioning dimensions materially redistribute `P(Y1,…,Yk|C)`, and which do not?
- What is the majority configuration? What are the supported minority co-travel alternatives and unusual counterpositioned combinations?
- What does a targeted recursive subdivision reveal, and does its full joint change or merely its marginal frequencies?
- What is an exact source-local comparison baseline and where is support absent?
- What observed exceptions are stable enough to merit follow-up, and where must inference be withheld?

**No uniform forced significance or causal interpretation.** The full joint, raw positives, exact denominator and feasible bounded re-query are valuable even before a source-specific analytic statistical court issues population interval estimates.

### IV.3. The population is not one person

The F5 observatory supplies population-configured *possibility*. It does not supply a preordained psychological subject. Multiple legitimate outcomes can exist at a given social position; the minority outcome can be the only historically plausible one for a particular fictional actor after independent individual history is constructed. A raw 60% answer does not make that person 60% committed.

There is an asymmetry: survey members may share an observed multiway profile *as respondents*; fictional parent and partner must not be assumed jointly co-drawn from a survey that never measured the same household, and cross-sectional waves are not their mutual childhood.

## V. Position without fetishizing variables: the independent social-position side

**Question:** Where is the respondent/population configuration *relationally* situated, and how does that constrain the distribution of orientations without turning either into a personality attribute?

N028's [Position Role Authority](../CORPUS/EXPLORATORY/f154bcad631b__09_POSITION_ROLE_AUTHORITY_CONTRACT_R1.md) proposes *claim-relative roles*, not intrinsic global variable identities. Its nine categories include:

- active position coordinate;
- candidate resource/capital proxy;
- relational identity position;
- trajectory/origin indicator;
- supplementary position descriptor;
- attitude/disposition-only;
- context/scope-only;
- administrative/ineligible;
- unresolved role.

A variable can have more than one valid **candidate interpretation** for different scientific claims. But at a specific comparison it cannot be simultaneously treated as an active position variable and an active disposition variable and then "discover" their homology. **Multi-role authority needs alternate builds, not double counting.**

Operationally retain source-local and context-specific position variables such as resource availability/deprivation, employment/occupation, education/credential, household reproduction, region/urbanity, religious/ethnic/caste/sectarian community as *relational position* where the instrument measures it—and objective property/control/institutional authority only when independently warranted. An ideological or moral attitude is not objective class.

**Crucial distinction:** the source's reported "middle class", occupation or income is a measured response/status claim. It is not a complete control-of-assets, rent/land, productive power or labor dependency relation. N6 separately qualified objective positions must be *derived from real independent evidence*, not constructed by naming an FF geometry "class".

The ontology can be a plurality of position views in an underobserved survey: economic resources, occupational relations, credentialed education, relational identities and household position. Do not force absent dimensions into a single fully populated social space.

### V-A. External evidence is an independent side of position constitution, not new respondent columns

The actual external estate now contributes materially distinct products:
- Population calibration: ILOSTAT/JOIN employment and working time, WID resource distribution, Findex financial capacity. These inform distributions and modeled missing coordinates; never resample a measured PYAT coordinate or independently multiply margins into a joint. JOIN and Findex selected values are native fractions, not 0–100.
- Relational qualification: EPR Core political group status/autonomy, ED composition, GeoEPR location scope, TEK transborder formations, RAS group/institutional differentiation and V-Dem public-institution conditions. Group status is not individual status, self-identification or actual exposure.
- Institutional conditions: WBL specific legal questions, RAS constitutional/regulatory evidence and independently grounded actual rules. Legal provisions qualify an institutional proposition, not the enforceable right of every particular person or the frequency of compliance. Actual material dependence additionally requires controller, affected participant, scope and resources.

ES01/03/04/08/10/12 actual native retrieval exists at R6 (01 II.K); ES09 RDS unexecuted here; other acquired families are metadata-level in this Browser. The exact 14-family selectors and semantics belong to the existing [source map](EXTERNAL_STRUCTURAL_EVIDENCE/01_SOURCE_TO_FUNCTION_MAP.md), not a new universal schema. Source-native evidence remains at its own grain, with separate observed/reference/effective/publication dates, native identities, measurement scope and permission. Source-local PYAT configurations remain respondent-coherent. A context correspondence links the independent sides when qualified; it does not create a new joint respondent measure. A national constant cannot be replicated over respondents and called a material position dimension.

If a requested position is already measured, preserve it. If missing and external distribution is compatible, supply a source-backed population constraint, not a personal fact; a downstream modeled completion remains explicit. Where multiple sources concern different populations, even ordinary joint bounds require compatible scope, and their incompatibility stays visible. Objective role/command remains separately constituted. A dependent contractor, own-account worker or small employer cannot be identified with a complete class relation from category alone. The selected query must retain uncertainty and partial roles without blocking all useful contextual interpretation.


## VI. Disposition without a magic ideology axis: an atlas of coadministrable relations

Original N028 hypothesized a rich local disposition geometry, but the later codebook stress test substantially corrected it:

1. Govern *coadministration topology* first; only risk-set connected measurements can legitimately inhabit a common metric.
2. Source-native questionnaire blocks, analytic-role blocks and same-respondent exposure blocks are three distinct things. Module size is not sociological importance.
3. Distinguish **exact joint repertoire** (the observed combinations) from **optional disposition geometry** (derived shape/position of variables/people under a specified estimand). They do not replace one another.
4. Some survey contexts produce several disconnected *local* disposition atlases and cannot be forced into one universal, complete ideological manifold.
5. Sparse/rare modalities must not disappear through majority smoothing merely because they complicate a neat embedding.
6. Projection into a measured dimension is not license to infer a psychologically acquired intention, especially for children or people outside the original survey risk set.

Where the joint is already sufficient, reconstruct it directly via I03/S04/FF01. Geometry is method-contingent: compare with an exact conditional baseline, and admit only a typed additional consumer value.

### VI-A. Complex identity, repertoire plurality and the missing relation between them

[E116's original contract](https://github.com/maidiu/rawlsing/blob/3065c22ab03be56796399d266856cbc7fa22f933/data/ddm_build_copy/recovery_integration/semantic_relation_R1_through_Job3/R1_20260825/FIELD_FORGE_R1/11_DISPOSITION_REPERTOIRE_AND_COMPLEX_CONTRACT_R1.md#L65-L253) distinguishes an item/proposition, a respondent-backed exact joint complex, and a wider repertoire. **A repertoire is not the union of every separately supported complex.** If complex A and complex B were each measured on different respondents, no co-possession between them follows. A wider co-possession claim must be proven at its own same-unit grain. Conversely, an exact configuration can contain several socially interpretable complexes, some in tension, without requiring a single latent ideology. The logical or semantic meaning of a contradiction is a separate assertion from the observation of co-occurrence.

Complex identity includes member propositions and response direction/morphology, same-respondent law, completion, exact scope, risk and configuration semantics. Changing a member, response scale or universe changes the evidence identity; a family of analogous variants requires its own qualified correspondence, not merely label or embedding similarity. Keep direct observed support, completion profile, intra-complex variation, minority alternatives, conditional distribution and uncertainty available to the actual query. A single prevalence or "complex strength" cannot replace them unless a specific lawful recipient's required information demonstrably survives. A highly prevalent repertoire and a highly probable configuration are not necessarily the same estimand.

[E112](https://github.com/maidiu/rawlsing/blob/3065c22ab03be56796399d266856cbc7fa22f933/data/ddm_build_copy/recovery_integration/semantic_relation_R1_through_Job3/R1_20260825/FIELD_FORGE_R1/05_THEORY_ENGINEERING_STACK_R1.md) identifies two distinct positive questions within this territory: symbolic/moral boundary judgments (deservingness, dignity, exclusion, worth) and justificatory repertoires. A generic policy preference cannot be relabeled a grammar of worth. Source/J/fracture/semantic owners supply the evidence; a qualified interpretive owner supplies the meaning when the actual question requires it. No common-context activation or enacted strategy follows from a cross-sectional repertoire. These are not separate mandatory artifacts; I03/S04/FF01 exact joint and discovery queries are the first comparison.


### VI-B. HULCH and the neglected formation of a differentiated social world

**R10 governing discipline (direct user direction, November 25):** FIELD FORGE HOME and G4 remain the primary commitment. MULCH THE LEAST OF THESE selects the neglected consequential whole-machine demand; MULCH YOUR NEIGHBOR lets actual adjacent construction inflect that choice, without transferring jurisdiction. HULCH makes that construction productive, self-correcting and capable of generating new concepts and inquiries rather than merely executing an inherited finite agenda. Consult the actual sources below. No FF CIC, new inventory, new branch, duplicate world engine or diversion of PYAT P0.

The positive target is **a revisable actor-independent field of socially available alternatives**, not a taxonomy of persons or a list of independently weighted strata. The two independent evidentiary sides are coherent response repertoires and material/institutional relations. Their juxtaposition becomes FIELD FORGE intelligence when it changes what can be understood, asked, or made operative. HULCH [§§VIII–XIV, XVI–XIX](https://github.com/maidiu/rawlsing/blob/638bb7a4a1049bd666946e869a7fde7592dd2624/PYAT_CONTROL_ROOM/PROGRAMMA_PYAT/DHIKRYMULCH/00_DHIKRYMULCH.md) supplies the deeper demand: do not merely classify known formations; discover, challenge, split, merge and refine candidate concepts in response to real observations. Keep this concept revision distinct from the constitution of actual objective positions and downstream psychic organization.

**A concrete productive inquiry circuit:**
1. The question may arise from an upstream discovery, external structural difference, or a downstream need. Specify what relation matters: e.g. whether abstract support for equality travels with selective restriction of actual autonomy in a particular social position, and which institutions reproduce that contradiction. Do not assume the concept is already a questionnaire field. Identify source item meanings, response-act roles (principle/application, norm/practice, end/means, commitment/cost, target selectivity), coadministration and source-local risk.
2. Retrieve actual existing PYAT joint/conditional/reverse projections and alternatives through I03/S04/I05/FF01 as applicable. Seek fractures, minority-exit branches, co-travel and higher-order conditions, not only the dominant answer or strongest splitter. Preserve source-native identities, support, uncertainty, response/eligibility and observed versus unsearched absence. No additional joint kernel. No claim that distinct source surveys observe the same persons.
3. A coherent observed motif may invite an LLM-proposed candidate social concept. Ask for its defining relations, counterexamples, scope, rival meanings, alternative measurements and the smallest discriminating query. Keep it provisional; failure can change the concept rather than merely assign another label. A response motif can be real without a corresponding independently constituted group. A stable candidate social kind is not a person's psychology. Do not promote a classifier label to objective class, hegemony or actual carrier practice.
4. Bring in independent material evidence at its native scope, e.g. actual EPR position and WBL institutional case, actual labor/protection distribution or explicit world-owned role. Determine whether a qualified correspondence exists; where absent, retain independent results and describe a modeled juxtaposition. Ask which controllers, resources, renewal conditions, access, practices, competing recognitions and costs could make the observed repertoire socially consequential. The source repertoire is not the mechanism, and a difference in prevalence need not change practical force.
5. Let the relation revise the inquiry. If the same repertoire appears under opposite positions, ask whether its practical expression, institutional carrier, targeted application or cost structure differs; if a supposed uniform formation splits when conditioning on another measured position, consider two concepts or a context-qualified concept; if the result disappears after exposure/risk correction, preserve the negative and withdraw the unsupported interpretation. A countercase can revise the social concept itself. An unresolved question can be a useful product, not a failed run.
6. Serve what remains useful downstream: source-supported alternatives and independent contextual relations, competing interpretations and qualified limits; explicitly modeled public practice or institutional possibilities when historical facts are undetermined. A modeled carrier or Other can realize one of several possible histories; actual exposure, agency, interpretation and acquisition remain with SOROK. No simulated society is required to instantiate all possible individuals. No observed joint is rewritten to suit a desired adult.

**Three forms of HULCH that must be real in this operation:**
- Concept birth and revision. The machine must be able to say that the old category hid two different configurations, or the proposed similarity was a measurement artifact. Preserve the same source facts when the concept changes. Proposed concepts and rival interpretations should remain addressable in existing inquiry/semantic products, not become new source facts. Finite documentary semantic adjudication may be frozen by the competent owner; open-ended abductive suggestions are distinct, testable, nonauthoritative proposals. A named role or repertoire remains revisable; social groups need not share a coherent ideology.
- Positive partial knowledge. HULCH successor #1193 distinguishes compatible-population constraints from individual joint observations, and finite eligible unresolved-response bounds from guesses about silent persons. If a known eligible set has 36 affirmative, 24 negative and 40 unresolved in 100, the completed-response range is 36–76%, not a proved majority or inferred inner beliefs. If compatible marginals are 70% and 60%, the overlap lies between 30% and 60%; the correlation and joint configuration remain unidentified. For incompatible populations do not calculate the bound. These are conceptual witnesses, not accepted native methods. Reuse existing F6/observation/silence/science owners and qualified outputs when available; don't make FF a partial-ID solver or synthesize respondent data. A fictional completion under a declared model is still possible without becoming observed truth.
- Productive negative and next discriminator. A comparison needs to expose which mutually incompatible accounts still fit evidence, which relevant unknown could change the modeled field, and what smallest lawful additional observation would distinguish them. A lawfully empty result is valuable; an unsearched question or unavailable source must not be declared absent. The explorer belongs upstream; FF supplies structurally meaningful discriminators and acts on returned evidence. No mandatory exhaustive search or unlimited budget.

**Worked conceptual circuit (fictional numerical illustration, no claim about any actual group):** suppose one actual future query could measure equality-principle endorsement alongside application to an outgroup and willingness to bear a cost. A formation can appear uniformly egalitarian when only the first item is compared. A joint reveals a principled-and-cost-bearing alternative and a principled-but-selective alternative. Rather than naming the latter hypocritical, consider rival explanations: different understood targets, a binding material dependency, public conformity, or distinct conception of equality. Source wording and exact coadministration can defeat the first apparent contrast. An independent institution may control access to employment or assistance and make public dissent costly; its actual existence and reproduction must be independently established or explicitly modeled, not inferred from survey response. A follow-up of application/cost conditional on the actual measured position can distinguish some rivals, while actual institutional practice remains separately evidenced. The generative world can make two different carriers of equality available, each organizing access differently, without determining how a parent or Nadia appropriates them. If later inquiry shows the category fails, revise the candidate concept and retain prior evidence. That is HULCH as constructive social intelligence, not one more fixed query label.

**What FF adds irreducibly:** interpretation and composition of relevant actor-independent relations between separately supported repertoires and material/social context, questions about field-relative possibility and reproduction, and alternative conditions usable in downstream historical construction. It does not own all empirical discovery merely because the conceptual machine is recursive. The demand is governed by existing G4 S06.1–.4 PP26-13–19/21; local geometry optional only where actual value beyond exact joint baseline. VZVKADD horizontal owner reconciliation still pending. Do not re-open general historical archaeology or displace P0. 


## VII. Local geometry is a possible method, not the constitutional object

A source may support MCA/GDA, categorical MDS, partial supplementarity or several linked local views. The historical FFX B implementation already calculates local distances, embeddings, neighborhoods, stress and jackknife stability. **That concrete existing code is to be reused before proposing another.**

Geometry requires a named **representation target/estimand**:

- Does it represent actual sampled respondent distribution, a target population under a purpose-specific weight, an ordinal relation, or source-limited categories?
- Which variables are active or merely projected supplementary?
- Are rare modalities an intended sociological signal or an accidental leverage point?
- Does one question battery dominate because it is large, not because the world gives it more weight?
- Are continuous/ordinal measurements discretized? How is information lost and the choice justified?
- Are source/country/risk-set/time comparable in this local view?
- How much reconstruction stress and bootstrap/leave-one-out instability attends a proposed neighborhood?
- Are design or calibration factors already associated with active position inputs, thus contaminating interpreted axes?

The original R1 vision correctly demanded **separate position and disposition spaces** before any claim of homology. The later E032 stress pass adds a stronger identification law: construct both independently; record active input ancestry; link later by exact respondent identity; run lawful permutation/cross-fit and measurement-regime sensitivity. A supervised method maximizing position↔disposition correspondence may be useful exploratory mathematics, but it is not independent evidence that the social world itself exhibits the discovered homology.

If sources lack an identifiable common metric, **keep separate objects and report no comparison**. Strong HOMOLOGY remains unissued absent its own authority.

### VII-A. What a method may add, and what it must not decide

[E115](https://github.com/maidiu/rawlsing/blob/3065c22ab03be56796399d266856cbc7fa22f933/data/ddm_build_copy/recovery_integration/semantic_relation_R1_through_Job3/R1_20260825/FIELD_FORGE_R1/10_LOCAL_POSITION_SPACE_CONTRACT_R1.md) and [E122](https://github.com/maidiu/rawlsing/blob/3065c22ab03be56796399d266856cbc7fa22f933/data/ddm_build_copy/recovery_integration/semantic_relation_R1_through_Job3/R1_20260825/FIELD_FORGE_R1/19_GEOMETRY_GOVERNANCE_AND_WEIGHTING_R1.md) require a geometry's declared context, roles, modality semantics, weight/design purpose, actual denominator, missingness treatment and stability. A region is a derived membership/boundary rule, not a primary class. High inertia/contribution is not sociological importance or cause. No rare modality is inherently noise or necessarily active; source-native rare identity remains traceable even if supplementary, and semantic collapse needs independent authority. If identity is an admitted structural coordinate, resource-only position with supplementary identity and full relational position with identity active are rival lawful views when the question calls for them—not two always-required models. Direct respondent placement and model-derived placement of incomplete/generated profiles have different provenance and estimands.

The original method/authority sequence (E114/E119/E120) has a legitimate falsification purpose: choose diverse existing contexts, reserve unlike held-out contexts, treat informative failure as a limit, and demand an actual consumer gain. No global geometry or compulsory historical FF-B/X sequence survives R2 merely because its original method was serious. If a direct relation supplies the same answer, do not build a second geometry. Historic FFX B is the first code reference where a real reader needs geometry; MCA/GDA, balanced block choice or a mixed-data method must be separately warranted.

[E125](https://github.com/maidiu/rawlsing/blob/3065c22ab03be56796399d266856cbc7fa22f933/data/ddm_build_copy/recovery_integration/semantic_relation_R1_through_Job3/R1_20260825/FIELD_FORGE_R1/24_POSITIONED_QUERY_WALKBACK_INTEGRATION_R1.md) states an especially consequential distinction: geometrically close positions can be non-substitutable for a target. One position dimension may be relaxable for an institutional-trust query and nonrelaxable for a moral-boundary query. Authorized walkback preserves target, retained/relaxed dimensions, loss, support, changed distribution and exact source authority; it must never silently erase an identity or cross source/release to obtain larger n. The existing protected positioned-subject possibility owner already evaluates exact support and authorized decreasing-subset proxy paths, preserving an unresolved exact lane when proxy is available. Whether the issued rules actually differentiate target families in the selected source/consumer is an authority/first-reader question, not an automatic new algorithm.

## VIII. Empirical pressure: reconfiguration is observable; causation is not magically supplied

Here the original N028 vision is most potent and most easily abused.

### VIII.1. Neutral footprint

Given source-admitted position configuration `C`, disposition-repertoire joint `Y` and lawful comparison `C'`, the **present #1247 FF01 research code** calculates *RAW complete-case descriptive support*, not an unqualified eligible-population probability. Let `n_cc(C,Y=y)` count fully resolved, same-respondent coadministered cells and let `n_cc(C)` count all fully resolved joint cells for that context. The descriptive quotient is defined only for positive resolved support. **The present FF01 implementation has a stricter entrance condition:** the entire *selected eligible producer risk* must be resolved and positive before any such conditional comparison is admitted; it rejects a partially resolved positive fact. Within that admitted joint the narrower conditional denominator may differ from the original selected eligible denominator. Then it yields

`p_cc(y|C) = n_cc(C,Y=y) / n_cc(C)`,

and similarly for `C'`. Its raw contrast `Δ_cc(y;C,C') = p_cc(y|C) − p_cc(y|C')` describes **respondents complete in the relevant joint**, not all eligible respondents and not a population estimand. The original I03 risk/eligible denominator remains a **separate field**. Missingness, route/exposure eligibility, unresolved values, weights, design, substantive response and source permission cannot be erased by renaming the complete-case ratio `P(Y|C)`.

A weighted/population `P(Y|C)`, missingness adjustment, identification bound or uncertainty interval is **a different operation**, requiring its explicit source-specific estimand, original authorizer, design/routing and adequacy law; **#1247 FF01 currently refuses weighted support and positive incomplete eligible-risk joints**. #1292 supplies upstream observation/silence and observed-cohort comparisons; it does not by itself convert held partial risk into positive FF01 evidence. When a context has no resolved support, preserve typed zero/held without inventing a fraction. Preserve the complete-case distribution and its negative states without treating its conditional difference as a coercive mechanism.

Candidate optional diagnostics include total variation, Jensen–Shannon distance, conditional mutual information, log-linear n-way terms and profile shifts across position-neighborhoods. None by itself proves coercive exposure, a school or family causal mediation, or that a person "internalized" the group's politics.

For instance, compare two employment-control positions with equal incomes. If source self-report does not record actual productive control, the direct descriptive **C must remain source-scoped and noncausal**; an independently admitted employer role may then change an N9 model's opportunities *without rewriting the measured survey distribution*.

### VIII.2. Nonadditivity / articulation

The position world is interactive. The distribution under race/sect, region, employment, household and institutional experience can change in ways that **cannot be reconstructed as the sum of isolated factor main effects**. A crucial question is whether `P(Y | A,B)` differs from what the available `P(Y|A)` and `P(Y|B)` would predict, *under the true same-person risk set*.

The method must preserve:

- exact higher-order cells and their denominators;
- reconfiguration that disappears when one condition is omitted;
- minority/counterpattern trajectories within a composite context;
- conditional reversals and competing cohorts;
- asymmetry of material power *only when sourced*, not inferred from a symmetric statistical interaction;
- nonidentification when the needed J combination was not actually administered together.

This is empirical **coupling** as a neutral typed statistic. An actor-independent "articulation" label or causal "buffer" requires additional theory/court. The reader can ask the stronger question without the answer being forged.

### VIII.3. Identity and social pressure do not coincide

Two people occupying similar reported income and education positions can confront very different enforceable terms: citizen versus precarious worker, landlord versus tenant, employer versus wage-dependent worker, kin-supported versus care-isolated parent, legally protected versus excluded minoritized group. **Income score is not power.** A meaningful full machine needs a pathway for *independently admitted material institutions and group relations* to qualify downstream opportunities—not an FF classifier that reads a demographic tuple and asserts a class destiny.

### VIII.4. Graded evidence and honest sparse fallback — inherited but not yet stitched

Original exploratory [E029](00_VISION_ARCHITECTURE_AND_PREIMPLEMENTATION_SOURCE_LEDGER.md) explicitly distinguishes **E0 direct adequately supported empirical evidence; E1 direct but underpowered; E2 model-supported partial pooling; E3 governed broader-position walkback/proxy; E4 model extrapolation**, plus separate no-evidence and routing/J/role refusal states. These are historical *proposed evidence grades*, **not accepted current ABI enum values** or an instruction to implement five new engines.

The full claim-conditioned answer contract must preserve **which route produced its value**: source-local direct count, source-design-qualified population estimate, explicit uncertainty, independently issued model, governed loss-bearing walkback, or extrapolation with no direct support. E1 must not become observed zero; E2 must not impersonate E0; E3 must disclose the exact position information lost; E4 must not become a survey-verified actor prior. Check existing G4 I03/I05, positioned-subject walkback, model/science and source authority **before** asserting any of these routes needs new FF code. No full E029-to-current-code parity or original-source first-consumer court has yet been demonstrated.

## IX. The political-economic and hegemonic problem

The early N028 R1 corpus makes a hard distinction: **hegemonic is not majority, subaltern is not minority**. Real power comprises institutionally defended relations, the ability to distribute, impose, recognize, exclude, withhold, reorganize and turn others' reproduction into dependence. A numerically small group can dominate property/state control; a prevalent worldview can coexist with politically subordinate subjects.

An exploratory *evidence profile* may legitimately include, **only where individually supported**:

- survey prevalence and the whole joint attitude/repertoire pattern;
- reach across separately measured source-local social positions;
- alignment of high-resource/status groups in *observed* survey data, with sample-tail caveats;
- an independently admitted political/economic/institutional group-power crosswalk, at exact geography/period;
- local symbolic defaultness, recognition/boundary/aspiration evidence where measured;
- competing and counterpositional repertoire patterns;
- recurrence through legally comparable time/source contexts;
- field-specific differential valuation and possible conversion candidates, with uncertainty and missing dimensions.

Without independently authorized group-power/elite alignment inputs, an observed profile must not pretend to answer "which class actually rules here?" EPR/GeoEPR or other external sources cannot be joined by similarity of group names alone. Data coverage of the very wealthy and institutions is an actual source limitation, not a license to impute one.

The **source-observed difference in resource valuation** is a possible neutral precursor. `CAPITAL`, `FIELD`, `CONVERSION`, `HEGEMONY` and `PRESSURE_PATHWAY` remain separate strong interpretive claims requiring an issuer. This HOME preserves the question and explanatory stakes while honoring R2's non-adoption of those strong nouns.

There is no one scalar hegemony score or total "progressiveness" axis, and political majority cannot turn into an objective power variable through model narration.

### IX-A. Reach, recognition, power, conversion and fieldhood are different relations

The recovered [hegemony evidence contract E117](https://github.com/maidiu/rawlsing/blob/3065c22ab03be56796399d266856cbc7fa22f933/data/ddm_build_copy/recovery_integration/semantic_relation_R1_through_Job3/R1_20260825/FIELD_FORGE_R1/13_HEGEMONY_SUBHEGEMONY_EVIDENCE_PROFILE_R1.md) and [capital/field contract E118](https://github.com/maidiu/rawlsing/blob/3065c22ab03be56796399d266856cbc7fa22f933/data/ddm_build_copy/recovery_integration/semantic_relation_R1_through_Job3/R1_20260825/FIELD_FORGE_R1/14_FIELD_CANDIDACY_AND_CAPITAL_CONVERSION_CONTRACT_R1.md) reveal why the material-social world cannot be represented by one opinion distribution. Consider two equally prevalent repertoires. One is spread across many positions; the other is concentrated in a socially organized locality. Either could be institutionally powerful or politically subordinate. A narrow orientation held in an empowered institution can condition opportunities quite differently from an equally rare orientation held by a powerless group. **Frequency does not measure the means of reproduction or enforcement.** Nor does an externally evidenced group-power relation tell us a group's members' private beliefs.

The original seven-axis evidence profile must remain separable: (1) prevalence at the qualified estimand; (2) cross-position reach including penetration into opposed positions; (3) alignment with high observed positions, not inferred elite status; (4) independently grounded political-power alignment; (5) geographic reach/concentration and separately warranted centrality; (6) comparable temporal persistence/expansion; (7) structured counter-complexes. Prevalence, breadth and alignment are different operations even if the same I03/S04 query is sufficient to compute several. None necessarily needs geometry. Leave an unavailable dimension unavailable; do not impute group-power alignment from the rest. Taken-for-grantedness/defaultness is also a distinct claim: broad reach, contradiction and persistence can nominate it, but actual unreflective defaultness is unobserved unless source semantics support that distinction. No mandatory total or universal winner.

**Subhegemony is not simply smaller hegemony.** Local recognition may coexist with national subordination; a practice may have positive standing in one arena and negative standing in another. Several overlapping local and wider complexes can coexist. The relevant question is whether the practice's carriers, normative reach, material institutional capacity, recognition and cross-arena availability actually differ at the requested scope. These relations are meaningful to later social life because they qualify which ways of acting may be legible, rewarded, costly or excluded. They do not say what a fictional child knows, values or actually encounters.

Likewise, a credential, network, skill, identity or practice is not capital merely because it predicts an answer. Differential resource association is a lawful neutral precursor, while **conversion** concerns the directed relation from source arena to target arena and a resource's actual changing efficacy there. Positive, weak, zero, negative/devaluing, asymmetric, unresolved and unobserved relations are not interchangeable. Where the project can only observe a reversal of association, preserve that reversal without asserting an exchange rate or mechanism. A real transfer, sanction, opportunity or recognition must be warranted at its own material/semantic scope. No universal socioeconomic or cultural-capital score.

The old field-candidacy question differentiates six evidential dimensions: internal differentiated positions; stakes/dispositional organization; differential valuation; partial autonomy; reproducibility; and boundaries/entry/membership where observable. This is not a necessary universal checklist for every simple conditional. It is the evidentiary challenge to **strong fieldhood** when a future issuer actually attempts that interpretation. A formation/arena may organize real practices without being relatively autonomous in the technical sense; a position region may be a useful empirical grouping without either designation. If a stronger name is rejected or unresolved, the qualified lower-level relation is not deleted. The direct evidence and the interpretation have separate histories.

All these positive questions are conserved; strong HEGEMONY, FIELD, CAPITAL/CONVERSION, HOMOLOGY and causal pressure pathways remain held under R2 without the specific competent issuer. This HOME neither resurrects a compulsory profile/field carrier nor converts a strong social claim into an automatic FF output. Use direct qualified source-local contrasts where sufficient, and preserve the independent material relation when the actual reader requires it.

### IX-B. Formations as interpenetrating relations, not independently weighted strata

The seven axes above remain distinct. Add the actual means of symbolic reproduction and field relevance, not a composite score. A person can inhabit kin, class, religious, ethnic, territorial, school, work and political formations whose relations overlap, nest, oppose and transform each other. National subordination may coexist with local command, and a group may contain class, gender and generational antagonisms. Relevant formation is determined by actual dependency, access, recognition, participation, goods, counterparty and changing history, not distance from a universal center. Do not confuse self-identification, classification by others, institutional membership, co-presence and source group correspondence.

For any theme distinguish (1) source-observed response configurations, (2) expected approval/sanction by others, (3) actual publicly performed norms/practices and (4) power to reproduce them through resource and recognition control. These need not covary. A publicly dominant norm may survive widespread private dissent; low-prevalence discourse may be institutionally enforced. The first is not transparent private psyche; the second/third may be measured if appropriate source items exist or explicitly generated as fictional practices; the fourth requires independent control or modeled rule. Actor appropriation is separate and downstream.

**Positive FF-specific operation:** on a selected position/repertoire/arena question, ask I03/S04 for exact source-local conditional/reverse profiles and supports; independently retrieve the contextual observation or qualified role relation at its own grain; apply a documented correspondence only within its scope; return the contrasted repertoires, the independent relation and its applicability, differing axes/unknowns, rival interpretations and a precise next empirical or structural question when useful. This is not a new statistical kernel. Without a group crosswalk, serve the two independent products and withhold the aligned claim. With compatible groups, same conditional support under different executive position is a real differentiated profile; not sufficient for strong historical hegemony. Treat recognition, conversion and conflict as their own relations and ask for evidence of how each is reproduced. Never replace the existing seven axes. The [R5 worked circuits](EXTERNAL_STRUCTURAL_EVIDENCE/04_INHABITED_SOCIAL_FIELD_CONNECTION_ARCHITECTURE.md#9-three-complete-constructive-circuits-explicitly-fictional-examples) are concrete design witnesses, not executed scientific results.

**Global/imperial scope:** preserve actual macro power as a potential independent structural relation. For persona construction represent rent, service, work, displacement, kin transfer or publicly circulating interpretation where it becomes consequential; distinguish true cause from experienced effect and explanation. Hidden ultimate owner with no difference to reachable conditions does not demand extra resolution for that life. If ownership changes future conditions or is an object of work/organizing/meaning, model the relevant channel or ground the actual named relation. Do not fabricate a real-world historical cause; don't let its unknown identity prevent coherent modeled material constraints. No requirement to resolve the entire ownership chain, no new acquisition mandate.


## X. Historical social variation without fictitious longitudinal people

The social field changes over time, but what is measurable depends on particular source/wave instruments. Field Forge can provide:

- source-wave-local position and attitude configurations as of period T;
- comparable/held difference evidence across independent cross-sections;
- explicit source/measurement/response/weight/domain transport maps, with falsifiers;
- evidence that some relational pattern recurs or fails to recur under admissible comparisons;
- uncertainty and noncomparability rather than forced narratives of progress.

It cannot infer a particular survey respondent's full developmental life from aggregate changes between 1995 and 2010. A fictional parent could have a developmental history later constructed by SOROK, but that must remain **fictional, specifically generated history**, not survey-measured panel data.

The downstream V553/F7 and actual historical world are responsible for interpreting temporality and change in a qualified structural/actor case. Field Forge supplies source-native historical *evidence* and comparison conditions.

### X-A. Mobility, composition and mismatch are not retrospective psychology

[E123](https://github.com/maidiu/rawlsing/blob/3065c22ab03be56796399d266856cbc7fa22f933/data/ddm_build_copy/recovery_integration/semantic_relation_R1_through_Job3/R1_20260825/FIELD_FORGE_R1/20_TRAJECTORY_HYSTERESIS_AND_POSITION_MISMATCH_R1.md) distinguishes origin proxy, current measured position, subjective position and realized habitus. The four descriptive relations are origin-current difference, current-subjective mismatch, resource-credential composition mismatch and disposition-position mismatch.
