Their appropriate comparison is the observed repertoire at the current position, the qualified reference repertoire at that position, and an origin-associated repertoire **only where origin was actually measured and comparison is lawful**. Same current positions can have different supported distributions conditional on different origin proxies; economic and credential trajectories may differ in direction. None of these facts issues an individual's retained origin disposition, false consciousness, hysteresis or cleft habitus. Age, period, cohort and a generation unit cannot be interchanged (E112/E113). Source/time and N9/V3 owners govern genuine history.

A parent offer may include this conditioned origin/current evidence when useful and authorized. It is not a life history. A child receives the independently enacted adult/family environment and its unfolding activities, not inherited survey answers.

## XI. The material-social information that must reach the actual world

A full theoretical field isn't completed by a bundle of population attitude ratios if downstream cannot answer which alternative really exists. The source/knowledge frontier therefore includes *independently grounded* objective material statements:

- role: employee, employer, controller of production, owner, dependent, caretaker, union representative, official, household member;
- relation: who can set hours, withdraw pay, impose sanction, offer care, demand duty, own or transfer an asset, speak for an institution;
- scope: to which entity, in which place/time/institution, under which legal/moral/practical conditions;
- material cost and binding constraint: time, labor, money, resource stock, travel, housing, care capacity and actual enforcement;
- contradicting role claims/authority and permission/negative states.

**This is a requirements interface to a competent structural/world owner, not a declaration that FF already calculates these social mechanics.** The protected objective-position materializer consumes an already-admitted `ObjectivePositionInput`; sourcefree N9/P41 already reads and applies a small set of structural values. SOROK already has world/labor/care/institution code. We must attempt the existing producer/read boundary before deciding whether a new specific source interpretation or adapter belongs in FF/N6/N9.

Material source roles cannot be made "empirical" by manufacturing an I03 respondent fact: I04's implementation genuinely requires `EmpiricalQuerySpec` and `EmpiricalFact`. Independent material observations have a different authorizing path.

This is the crossing from *descriptive social configuration* to *materially inhabited social relations*, while keeping empirical law, source conjecture, world actuality and fictional actor knowledge separate.

## XI-A. Positive constitutive operation — the actual actor-independent machine, not a list of forbidden errors

The review was correct: making all empirical-to-psychology shortcuts illegal does not itself generate a substantive social field. **Field Forge's distinct productive task is to conserve and interrogate source-grounded social relationships in a form a later world/possibility reader can use without inventing people.** The machine must be concrete enough to answer a named request, but its September R2 constitution rejects requiring any single master object, graph, coordinate space or cache.

The source-by-proposition recovery below sharpens the operation into *eight logically distinct calculations/receipts*. A qualified minimal service may satisfy each by a direct existing producer query rather than issuing a new artifact.

| Constitutive act | Input that earns the calculation | Actual positive output to demand | If source is absent / what an actual first reader would lose | Existing owner, not a new automatic build |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **1. Determine what could have been observed** | Exact source, wave, form/split, route, respondent exposure and J/coadministration relation | Context capability and respondent exposure/risk topology; positive/blocked/unresolved dimensions separately; declared denominator and missingness | A question's appearance in a questionnaire cannot be interpreted as shared respondent observation. A valid absent risk returns a negative, not a generated answer. | S01/S02/C11/I03/I05, consumed by FF; E031.01–04 |
| **2. Retain real socially situated repertoires** | An actual same-respondent joint with exact response morphology, source authority, permission, risk and currently admissible estimand | Complete observed configuration support and truthful local conditional/reverse distributions; nonmodal, contradictory and zero/held branches | Without a k-way joint, a fourth condition cannot be sampled by multiplying observed pairwise margins; say held or request genuine cumulative query. | S04/FF01 existing #1247 candidate, not new sampler; E022.03/E026.03/E030.05 |
| **3. Establish the analytic *position role*** | Exact measured resource/occupation/education/identity/origin item plus independent claim-specific role authority | Source-local position/configuration manifest: active, supplementary, context, proxy, trajectory or unresolved; no fabricated power claim | A standardized income or sect label does not produce productive control; source confidence does not license theory by itself. | Existing source semantic/role owners and N6 when qualified; E024.01–10 |
| **4. Compare lawful alternatives** | Two or more common-risk, common-estimand conditioned sets with explicit comparator and method | Actual per-branch redistribution, sign/reversal, minority persistence, nonadditivity, evidence route and uncertainty; unsearched/not-comparable distinguished | If no qualified common cohort, emit refusal or set-valued answer. The test is source observed possibilities, not a population-mean pseudo-person. | S04 query/FF01 descriptive; conditional geometry only if needed; E026.01–11/E029.01–09/E030.01–10 |
| **5. Construct geometry/homology only *as demanded*** | Independently frozen position and disposition roles; common respondent link, no active ancestry overlap; exposure/weight/design/measurement regime | Scoped independent views and post-hoc correspondence with diagnostic grade (overlap, permutation null, holdout, regime and weight sensitivity). Exact repertoire is still available below the view. | If direct joint answers the question, do not generate MCA. If disconnected split ballots, refuse a common metric. No source-issued HOMOLOGY by default. | Existing FFX historic geometry at historical grade; R2 claim-specific representation; E028/E031/E032 |
| **6. Qualify actual objective material relations** | Independently warranted organization, person or group, resource, command, obligation, sanction, care relation, period and right-to-use; **not** a fabricated EmpiricalFact | Typed controlling/controlled roles, applicable right/obligation, alternatives and source/unknown authority, ready for an actual world consumer; contradictions and contested institution claims retained | An attitude profile alone cannot identify who owns land, can fire a worker or imposes childcare loss. No verified external issuer ⇒ the objective rule stays HELD, although descriptive FF01 stays usable. | Separate competent objective/world source owner → N6/M4/M5/N9; I04 only claims about a real EmpiricalFact. Source→world end-to-end proof still absent; E020/E032.13–15/R2 |
| **7. Compare contexts and historical conditions** | Distinct source-local estimands and external group/measurement/time crosswalk with explicit relation type (exact/subset/partial/aggregate/non-equivalent) | Typed comparator and allowed operations, honest bounds, temporal evidence and source-specific differences without a fake panel identity | Shared national label, weight column or coordinates cannot authorize a joined respondent or changing person's childhood. | R2 transport; existing F6/F7/V553–V558 when lawfully issued; E031/E032.13–14 |
| **8. Serve the next actor/world question** | Named consumer request with place, time, position, permitted use, required empirical and objective claims and current source root | Minimum response: which alternatives are source-supported; under which objective rules are they feasible or costly; which unknowns prohibit stronger statements; exact refs to requery and negative restoration | Rich prose with no change in an actual world option is not an applied material field. A published source key alone does not prove a SOROK actor encountered or learned from it. | N9/N10E/N10W first reader, later SOROK *owns* selection, encounter, activity and psychology; R2 firewall |

**The positive structural-power problem remains real.** Its constitutive object is not just the rule in force, but the historically produced relation through which that rule, the resources it controls and the positions it differentiates can continue or be transformed. The following account makes that distinction operative. For an independently evidenced rule R, an objective position p, action a, time t and place w, the necessary response is conceptually: rule applicability to p in w/t; controlled resource and gatekeeper; conditions for a; permitted, denied, contingent or disputed access; resource/time burden and sanction if warranted; source and conflict grade. This is a *required information question*, not a declaration that a universal FF permission-vector engine already exists. Today's protected N9 applies five modeled training/civil fields, and SOROK has a separate labor/care economy. Whether a competent external producer can qualify an employer/care fact for their actual selected reader remains **UNPROVED**. Do not fabricate an I04 empirical fact, grant actor rights from a survey, or build a second SOROK world.

### XI-A.1. Social reproduction: the conditions of existence are themselves social relations

A field is not a snapshot of individuals distributed over resources and opinions. Its positions exist through organized activities that produce and reproduce the means of living and the relations of command over them. Wage labor reproduces not only household purchasing capacity but also, under determinate property and labor relations, the employer's command over future labor; unpaid care reproduces capacities without necessarily giving the caregiver control of the resources that sustain that activity. A collectively governed provision can reproduce a capacity for refusal instead. The same immediate material stock can therefore sustain different relations of dependence. This is the constitutive substance of D010.02/D010.06, the immediate D100 formation inheritance and [DHIKRYMULCH XI](https://github.com/maidiu/rawlsing/blob/86a12c5f733d6a74b800e1bc3bdccb3ceba25e78/PYAT_CONTROL_ROOM/PROGRAMMA_PYAT/DHIKRYMULCH/00_DHIKRYMULCH.md#L358-L386) (candidate intellectual source, not adopted science). The latter's reproduction-channel and carrier-transformation concepts survive as information demands, not mandatory new persistent objects.

**Reproduction and dependency inquiry.** For a consequential relation, establish separately: (a) what capacity, institution, resource or relation is reproduced; (b) whose activity, transfers and resources sustain it, including unpaid work; (c) control of allocation, renewal, exclusion and surplus; (d) beneficiaries and cost bearers, including externalized costs; (e) which other relations depend upon its continuation; (f) collective or individual capacities that can change the terms; (g) conditions under which a change propagates, is absorbed, or becomes a new dependency. Retain directed counterpart, role, effective period and separately warranted claim type. A dependency claim need not imply a quantified effect or a necessary causal outcome. Distinguish a documented arrangement, a proposed mechanism, a modeled transition and an observed transformation; never infer any of them from an income category alone. A snapshot of eligibility and an account of the conditions of its continuation are different information products.

**Worked discriminatory world (explicit hypothetical, no native evidence).** Hold household income, hours, childcare places, cost and employment opportunities constant. In A, childcare continuation is at the employer's discretion and contingent on employment; workers have no allocation control. In B, workers collectively control a pooled fund, with an independently specified reserve and allocation rule. Both initially provide identical care. Under labor withdrawal, A's specified rule permits the employer to terminate care, reducing the household's practical capacity to sustain withdrawal; in B the reserve can preserve care within its stated limits and thereby protect that capacity. Under prolonged loss of contributions, B too may exhaust its reserve or collectively change provision; its future is not guaranteed. A change in the terms of work can thus change care, which in turn changes the conditions of labor and bargaining. The model must distinguish *control of reproduction* from mere availability, and the ability to reproduce dependence from the capacity to contest it. No outcome follows without the specified mechanism, resource duration and applicable transition law. The same empirical attitudes can be held fixed throughout. An actual world transition is not an identified original-world causal effect.

**Necessary computational answer:** identify the independently warranted conditions of renewal and dependency, compare conditional consequences of perturbing them, return changed or unresolved resource/role/eligibility relations with their antecedents, and retain the possibility of feedback into future conditions. The downstream world owner applies an actual rule to actual circumstances; SOROK enacts activity, cost, relationships and reconstitution. For example, #1272 at `731f71b61fad5764eb021776ae937a376a54e8a8` has reviewer-inspected candidate labor withdrawal, wage-derived relief, relief transfer, care effort and petition eligibility; see [independent review](https://github.com/maidiu/rawlsing/pull/1289#pullrequestreview-6033414824). Those are existing world operations, not justification for another FF economy. FF's contribution is to make the relation's constitution and the consequences of changing its conditions intelligible and addressable; competent material issuer and general source-to-world applicability remain held. Reproduction channels may be served by existing typed claims or qualified queries, not a compulsory graph or universal transition engine.

### XI-A.2. Relational field relevance: institutions transform, not merely transmit

A social arena matters to a position through determinate relations of dependency, recognition, access, activity and conversion. It does not matter merely because a population inhabits the same geographical container, or because a fixed hierarchy places it above another. The earlier dynamic-strata problem survives without the nine-strata ontology. Ask: *which* arena becomes practically consequential to this position, through *whose* command over *which* resource, with *what* entry conditions, conversion paths and alternatives? This question applies to the relation among arenas themselves, not only their separate observed distributions.

Institutions are active transformers: they select and exclude, allocate resources, demand or certify competence, organize practices, translate norms into conditions of access, reward or sanction, provide protection, reproduce relations or become sites of contestation. Several contradictory transformations can coexist. A charity can buffer hunger while making continued relief contingent on conformity; a union can convert collective dues and organization into material independence from individual employer discretion. Its declared norms, actual activities, documented rules, resource flows, observed orientations and effects must remain distinct. Directional value is relation-specific: a referral can be valuable to a worker seeking admission while the worker's credential has little value to the referring gatekeeper; a change in admission rules can devalue the referral without changing anyone's stated belief. The organization of reproducible practical alternatives, not prevalence alone, is indispensable to a hegemonic analysis. Strong HEGEMONY, FIELD or CONVERSION issuance still requires competent evidence and interpretation; neutral descriptions of control, access and valuation survive without those labels.

**Worked discriminatory world (explicit hypothetical).** Hold tuition, application rights, qualifications, religious and secular attitude distributions, employer membership and formal educational opportunity fixed. In P, a patron's referral controls admission and employers condition recognition on the patron-linked credential. In I, admission and recognition are independently assessed and the patron cannot allocate either. For a qualified applicant without a referral, the opportunity under P is contingent or blocked according to its specified rule; under I the referral is not a necessary condition. The patron institution's relevance and a referral's directional conversion value differ despite identical population marginals. Change only the referral rule: the conditional material dependence changes; measured attitudes need not. A possible Other from that institution is not an actual encounter, and the applicant's allegiance, resistance or psychic acquisition is not determined by the dependency.

**Necessary computational answer:** identify applicable directed links among positions, institutions, resources and rules, the transformation of access/value through those links, rival/alternative channels, scope and typed unknowns, and consequences of changed links. Distinguish existence of institution, formal eligibility, practical reachability, institutional activity, source-observed repertoire, possible Other, actual encounter and actor appropriation. Empirical source-local contrast can investigate relevant exposures; it cannot invent patronage or contact. N6/M4/N9 and the independent world/actor owners qualify and apply the actual circumstances. Preserve this dynamic relation even where optional geometry, a global field index or graph is not selected.

### Four dissimilar downstream necessity courts

The downstream filter must compare each proposed richness against the **simplest already lawful source operation**.

| Consumer request and rival architectures | Minimal truthful FF response | What distinct historical material adds only if a direct query starves the recipient | Falsifier / loss |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Cultural contradiction:** SOROK asks for two plausible adults with incompatible-but-observed combinations of trust, religion, work and moral boundaries in a particular respondent universe | Real I03/S04 observed joint cells, including minority and nonresponse, plus source/risk/complete-case grade, with actual SOROK draw separately | E026/E030 nonadditive comparison and E028/E032 geometry only if asked to explain *how patterns reorganize across position neighborhoods* and simple multiway query cannot answer | Same pair marginals but different triple produces different answers. A cluster/axis cannot invent a person’s motive. |
| **Material subordination:** position W must seek childcare while employer E controls leave and sanction | A separately issued material relation: actual rule/role/resource/applicability/conflict; independently observed repertoire remains separate | E020 field-specific valuation, E032 group crosswalk, E023 structural interpretation may expose a genuine relation *if independently sourced*; statistical class proxy cannot substitute | Change an admitted sanction, hold opinion joint fixed; world eligibility/cost should change. If only a document hash changes, the bridge is not proved. |
| **Historical transformation:** compare religious/educational relations in distinct wave/source periods without a panel | Two source-local estimates and typed compatible or incompatible comparator, with measurement, geography, design and time reference | E031 estimand choice and E032 alignment tests distinguish real change from instrument/weight differences; separate external material-history records may alter objective rules | Similar coordinates or harmonized label alone must not yield one longitudinal respondent or a unique change mechanism. |
| **Developing person:** Nadia's world includes parent, neighbor, school, peers and unequal opportunities before she can know those opportunities | Warranted actor-independent institutions, social positions, available repertoires and source boundaries; no invented child-survey measurement | Adult-survey role/trajectory and local valuation can constrain a fictional parental possibility; childhood peer culture and psychic acquisition are SOROK-owned active history | Change candidate social surroundings without actual encounter: focal psyche need not change; source-level possible Other is not an encountered one. |

### Information requirement versus chosen physical or mathematical representation

**Necessary:** source/routing/J exposure semantics; full observed joint and minority support where queried; original eligible versus complete-case risk; explicit position role; a comparison reference; correct direct/model/walkback evidentiary tier; external group/structural source compatibility; claim-specific nonpositive refusals; exact information fit for N9/N10/SOROK.

**Contingent methods/representations:** a persistent exposure atlas, MCA/specific MCA/MFA, a unified pressure-profile carrier, motif graph, local homology object, hierarchical pooling model, symbolic field/capital/hegemony interpretation or new storage representation. E031 demands the information *observable through* an exposure topology but explicitly warns not to create another manual exposure authority when original routing/J is already known. E029 demands the evidence route, **not** automatic construction of every pooling estimator. September R2 rejects a mandatory master carrier, **not** the ability to answer the question a carrier would have answered.

**Historical opposition:** The rejected option of reducing class to income, sect to power or hegemony to a majority would impoverish the material world. Equally, resurrecting the August R1 all-geometries waterfall as a prerequisite to any working field would make original-source validity compete with actual construction. Preserve each serious problem; test the current incumbent function before selecting another producer.

**These courts do not claim any executed integration tests.** They specify what would discriminate useful FF machinery from structurally correct but downstream-inert paperwork. Where the needed external issuer does not exist or has not been located, the right product is a precise held/unissued output and an identified owner—not a fictional material fact.

### XI-B. Semantic capital as a durable resource, not a new court for every query

The recovered [precompute-before-automate contract E124](https://github.com/maidiu/rawlsing/blob/3065c22ab03be56796399d266856cbc7fa22f933/data/ddm_build_copy/recovery_integration/semantic_relation_R1_through_Job3/R1_20260825/FIELD_FORGE_R1/21_PRECOMPUTE_BEFORE_AUTOMATE_AUTHORITY_MAP_R1.md) calls for high-intelligence adjudication once when a meaning-bearing surface is finite and enumerable, then normal deterministic consumption and replay. Its eight original candidate surfaces—position role, axis/region, complex interpretation, field/formation, conversion, hegemony, symbolic/moral boundaries and temporal interpretation—are **not eight issued owners or eight compulsory banks**. Existing source/role/I04/M1–M5 and learned/currentness owners must be checked first. A frozen meaning is a reusable claim-specific result, not an excuse to ask an LLM to regenerate it per actor. A novel meaning can be proposed, but requires its proper issuer; changing determinant source semantics, route, joint authority, universe, geometry or transport invalidates only the affected conclusion and its dependents. The original proposed gold/held-out/hostile false-promotion tests matter if automating the act of semantic issuance, not for every ordinary query. Compute may nominate a surprising relation; it may not make a social noun true by naming it. This preserves the original distinction between the expense of forming semantic capital and the cost of using it.

### XI-C. The native material source → actual world connection

R6 returns actual source-native value bodies and explicit retrospective, caller-selected scope. This is a new available producer, not a new source of authority. The first concrete structural operation must distinguish the source proposition from its particular world application: e.g. WBL's specific paid-leave question and duration, its standardized legal case, the selected fictional worker/employer/household, the modeled applicable entitlement, actual benefit and payment timing, care/time/resources and the resulting options. The source cannot identify the worker or automatically create a benefit. A leave duration can qualify a modeled rule; the actual work/care owner determines feasible alternatives with joint resource constraints. If the source proposition is not qualified, a separately modeled arrangement may still be used at fictional grade; never label it observed.

Similarly, employment partition and financial margins may calibrate an unmeasured circumstance without rewriting a measured PYAT configuration. A Findex report of emergency funds possible is not cash nor a joint saving/borrowing relation. EPR/ED/TEK may situate a public or Other formation but not issue actual contact. Retain the exact evidence and alternative, then the appropriate role or rule issuer establishes which relation holds for a particular world. N6/M4/M5/N9/N10W and actual material owners remain distinct; existing TEST_ONLY selected-world catalog and same-source guard are not general external ingress. J04's BEN0002/Q73A path is narrow. Do not weaken those guards to manufacture integration.

A useful world can be built with explicitly modeled particulars under the correct owner even when stronger historical attribution is held. Different worlds with equal empirical repertoire can organize work/care control differently; one household may depend on an employer, another on a collectively controlled reserve. Material options change; the actor's response remains open. Distinguish resource stock, authority to allocate, timing, renewal, alternatives, actual enacted rule and who knows what. Actual shared-budget/role/eligibility constraints and real readout, not a convincing explanatory story, make the construction computationally operative. 


## XII. The full question-to-capability machine: conceptual demand, discovery and engineering selection

**Correction to the former stopping rule:** a functioning present consumer is not a prerequisite for recognizing an indispensable informational demand of the intended organism. Nor is an already-formulated query the limit of inquiry. [FFIF012D](https://github.com/maidiu/rawlsing/blob/334b354c5494d41c3904e2d26f7a708d46aac403/data/ddm_build_copy/recovery_integration/semantic_relation_R1_through_Job3/R1_20260825/FIELD_FORGE_R2/REIDEATION/FFIF012D_DOWNSTREAM_CONSUMER_VALUE_STOPPING_R1/00_WORKER_RETURN.md) distinguished sufficiency for a frozen known claim from unknown-claim discovery, recurrence and replay; its sixteen classes are representation-neutral demands. September R2's adopted closure rejects unconditional representational richness, not future or emergent demands. Separate four judgments: (1) conceptual necessity for an intended process or for discovery, (2) admissibility and evidence/authority grade of a particular answer, (3) actual inherited and contemporary implementation/reader, (4) engineering selection of the weakest sufficient lawful path. A necessary distinction can be retained while its issuer or implementation remains held; a richer method may be warranted by discovery yield even when it adds nothing to a frozen query. Do not build it merely because it is conceivable.

| Downstream question | Existing answer pathway / intended FF contribution | Stronger representation only if | First receiving owner |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **1. Structural alternatives and constraints** | Qualified observed context/role contrast, source-backed or hypothetical material relation/permission, typed negative | Exact weaker relation cannot express a selected actual constraint | Structural N6/N9 |
| **2. Configuration dependence** | I03/S04/FF01 full k-way `P(Y|D)`; source-replay | Higher-order state too large or actor consumer needs retained index/motif | N10E and C4/C5 |
| **3. Contradiction/counter-pressure** | Joint positives, counterpattern, nonadditive descriptive interaction without scalarization | Named structural higher-order relation separately admitted | V555/V556/N9 |
| **4. Relational/contextual location** | Exact source position role and local/partial view, not demographic essence | Geometry needed for a named rule and its estimand | N6/N10E |
| **5. Scope/applicability** | Source version/risk set/participant/time/permission and typed denial | New source/group crosswalk proven necessary | M4/M5/N9 |
| **6. Temporalization prerequisites** | Comparability/recurrence under source-local time scopes | Separate temporal state/transition authority | V553/F7 |
| **7. Mediation prerequisites** | Descriptive conditional pattern and qualified antecedent/source | Causal mechanism independently admitted | V555/SOROK |
| **8. Higher-order route prerequisites** | N-way jointly measured interaction and coherent alternative evidence | True V556 interaction law and owner need | M3/V556 |
| **9. Recurrence/transport** | Local_A, local_B, typed map, comparator/falsifier or NO_TRANSPORT | More retention needed for actual repeated cold queries | Cross-source/F7 |
| **10. Repertoire/alternative availability** | Complete supported multiway states and exact off-modal alternatives | Dense motif index warranted by first reader | N10E/C4 |
| **11. Minority/counterpattern** | Source-positive rare cells, denominator/privacy and explicit backoff | Qualified pooling/counterfactual estimator required | N10E/FF conditional |
| **12. Typed absence/abstention** | Zero vs held vs missing/coadmin unresolved vs refused | Never convert to filled world truth | Every recipient |
| **13. Provenance/replay** | Exact producer source, permission, query, relation and reconstructability | Physical cold-index required for performance | I03/M4/M5 |
| **14. Unknown-claim discovery** | I05 candidate search, query replay, selective unknown predicates | Retained graph only if direct query demonstrably starves | I05/FF |
| **15. Empirical→structural admission firewall** | I04 named claim requires observation + rule; separate external material source route | Strong law issued by competent court | N5/N6 |
| **16. Producer-preserving publication/resolution** | Typed exact relation reference for V557/V558 without copied issuer | Directly proven missing serving operation | M4/M5 |

**Computational norm:** compare the simplest lawful incumbent first. For a frozen claim the decisive test is sufficient information delivered to the intended reader and, when executable, its changed result. For discovery the relevant test is additional qualified discriminating questions, countercases or revision of an interpretation, not necessarily added truth of a previously frozen claim. A richer object is not an end in itself.

### XII-A. Reciprocal empirical–structural inquiry, not a one-way table-to-meaning pipeline

The field is an object of inquiry that can disclose inadequacies of the questions with which it was approached. Empirical regularities nominate competing structural interpretations; independent institutional knowledge nominates discriminating empirical questions; observation, silence and measurement critique can modify the interpretation, the next inquiry or the claim's admissibility. None of these steps gives an LLM permission to alter an issued instrument meaning. Novel semantic hypotheses can be proposed, adjudicated by the competent issuer, retained and consumed, with invalidation of affected dependents when warranted. This is the operative relation in DHIKRYMULCH XI's carrier/observation/competing-explanation loop, E025/E027/E031 and FFIF012D's unknown-claim discovery, not an additional scheduler.

**Worked inquiry (hypothetical responses, no empirical result asserted).** Suppose an observed association between religious participation and reported obedience occurs among workers. Rival accounts include patron-mediated access to employment and a repertoire that varies with other features of occupation or community. A separately warranted institutional record showing referral-based hiring in one arena but not another suggests a discriminating question: among comparable positions and actual eligible/coadministered respondents, compare the joint of participation, reported referral-mediated access (only if the instrument measures it), employment dependency and obedience across those regimes; preserve minority configurations, role and measurement differences. A same-risk contrast that persists specifically in referral-conditioned access is evidence consistent with, not proof of, the first account; it motivates further tests of alternative explanations and temporal or independently documented mechanism evidence. If the difference disappears, do not issue the proposed institutional interpretation as an empirical finding; revise or retain it as a competing hypothesis. If referral questions were asked only in one form or regime, #1292's observed-scope/cohort comparison may reveal noncomparability: the next operation becomes a source-qualified narrower common-risk inquiry or an explicit unidentified distinction, not a false cross-form causal contrast. If no instrument measures referral, keep the institutional claim separately warranted and ask what observable implication is genuinely measured; do not create a referral column or construe silence as fear. A novel counterpattern can in turn nominate a new question about alternative channels of access, without waiting for a completed actor reader to request it.

Existing S04/I03/I05 (including protected adaptive scheduling, G4 `G3A:CURRENT:G1B-F-D-013`) own qualified requery and exploration; S05 owns learned lifecycle; #1292 is existing observation/silence candidate, not a new authority. FF must formulate relation-specific discriminators and consume qualified answers, not duplicate discovery execution. The desired loop can operate through direct queries and governed hypothesis/meaning references. A future discovery representation is selected only if it materially improves qualified discovery/replay over existing operations; no universal carrier, new authority or automatic strong claim follows.

## XIII. A representative full circuit, showing where Field Forge ends

Imagine a historically situated survey population with measured household resource, employment, institutional trust and family-duty responses, plus an *independently modeled sourcefree* parent-world situation. All numerical values below are illustrative SOURCEFREE test conditions—not original-survey observations.

**Empirical:**
- I03/source authenticates which same respondents answered household resource `D`, institutional trust `T`, childcare-duty orientation `Y` and (if measured) labor situation `L`.
- FF01 computes `P(T,Y|D,L)`, preserves exceptions and exact source risk; if `L` was not coadministered, it refuses the false four-way joint.
- Alternate demographic strata with identical one-way support but opposite two-way/triple interactions remain different substantive empirical worlds. The later fictional adult is not pre-assigned the modal combination.

**Objectively modeled material context:**
- A separately qualified sourcefree **employer E** can deny schedule flexibility to worker P under a condition; caregiver C is available 0 versus 3 hours; child N requires supervision. A co-worker, institution or collective K may offer support, sanction or organized resistance.
- These rules are not extracted as "cause" from survey `P(Y|D,L)`. The real owner/world records them with source/claim/permission and holds where no authority is available.
- Existing N9/SOROK world machinery tests options: accept shift, seek care, ask for leave, refuse work, withdraw labor, use a fund. Cash without caregiving time is not automatically a substitute for care; a working spouse does not mechanically mean available care.

**First reader and actor:**
- N9/N10W combines matched legitimate empirical alternatives with *independently* applied world possibilities; if the relevant employer relation changes, real eligibility/cost should change without changing a fixed FF01 empirical joint.
- SOROK first selects/constrains the particular parent and, through actual social practice, develops separate mother/father/child histories. The parent might obey, bargain, lie, pray, resist or accommodate; the choice is not a statistical personality assignment.
- Nadia's actual reception may differ from her parent's intention. She might learn a practical routine, a false analogy or nothing psychologically durable. That is **SOROK**, not an FF tuple.

**Verification:** change the source's genuine observed four-way cell and check the FF descriptive output differs; separately change the employer world rule and check N9/SOROK material options differ. Swapping one without changing the other must not contaminate the other layer. Then inspect an actual later SOROK state read, not a convincing narrative. A false FF→world or world→psyche ownership transfer is RED.

**No scenario monopoly:** the 2026-11-22 [CO R13 later HOME](https://github.com/maidiu/rawlsing/blob/0d610622c247ab62541165e246805c38f21315d2/SEAM/30_THREE_AGENT_MULCH_20261119_R1/00_START_HERE_THE_CONSTITUTIVE_ORGANISM.md) explicitly warns against elevating labor organization, sanction or any dramatic case to the constitutive center. Work/care is a falsifiable first-reader specimen here, not the definition of human social life. The same field capability must admit quiet play, kinship, religious practice, ordinary consumer choice, migration, love, boredom and institutional relations when lawful source evidence exists. **This circuit is the target function, not proof the whole runtime already executes it end-to-end.**

## XIV. Hard adversarial architecture and evidence tests

1. **Pairwise parity trap:** two same-person triple joints agree on every one- and two-way margin yet disagree on `P(Y|A,B)`; the returned FF evidence must distinguish them.
2. **Exposure topology trap:** a source has two respondent-disjoint randomized forms; no four-way shared psychological complex may be asserted across them.
3. **Follow-up routing trap:** source-column present but respondent not eligible, should not become a genuine negative response.
4. **Multi-answer trap:** repeated/select-all distinct valid endorsements must not be silently coerced into scalar-singular false co-travel.
5. **Cartesian explosion trap:** a genuine supported sparse high-dimensional query must be tested against existing ≤16,384-cell FF01 capacity; if held, do not invent dense probabilities.
6. **Minority and privacy trap:** a genuine rare positive survives within lawful release rights; a privacy-restricted case denies, both without turning held into observed zero.
7. **Respondent-axis trap:** new joint dimension requires original coadministered I03 evidence; no joining separate marginal tables on column names.
8. **Weighted estimand trap:** sample geometry and target-population geometry cannot be named identical without weight/design authority.
9. **Homology ancestry trap:** an attitude used as position coordinate must be excluded or supplementary in disposition space in the primary independent test.
10. **Homology null trap:** apparent cross-space correspondence dies under legitimate held-out/permutation/relevant measurement-mode controls: retain negative evidence, not HOMOLOGY.
11. **World group-power trap:** equal survey prevalence and different separately sourced institutional power may produce equal **observed** attitude frequency but different applicable material-world constraints; do not overwrite either.
12. **False hegemony trap:** 60% answer prevalence cannot issue hegemony without independent power/field and competent semantic admission.
13. **Coupling asymmetry trap:** symmetric observed A–B interaction is not proof both exert equal control or that either causes the other.
14. **Conditional backoff trap:** `36 YES / 24 NO / 40 unresolved` out of 100 eligible does not justify treating unresolved as NO or a fixed 36% population outcome; support/identified bounds or truthful held state required at exact measurement grade.
15. **Temporal splice trap:** a 1995 survey and a 2010 survey are not one respondent's 15-year biography; NO_TRANSPORT if source bridge absent.
16. **Objective role trap:** same cash resources, employer asset control vs wage dependence; FF observed source may not claim objective role, while independent N6/world can differentiate it.
17. **I04 pseudo-source trap:** independently modeled employer rule must never pass by inventing an I03 `EmpiricalFact`.
18. **World substantive value trap:** changed permission or care hours must change actual world action eligibility/cost, not only hash of a package.
19. **Person leakage trap:** independent Other's unknown/unperceived actual event cannot auto-update Nadia's internal knowledge or acquired PRACTICE.
20. **Cold first consumer trap:** a computed but never read FF relation or world row counts as information capital, not full functional closure; test actual selected recipient from cold state.
21. **Authority-old-code trap:** code present on #1247 or historical FFX cannot be described as protected unless received; an existing protected file cannot be called missing because stale G4 says so.
22. **Cost trap:** full joint materialization needs measured time/storage/cold-reopen economics relative to legal re-query or sufficient statistics, not an automatic cache graph.
23. **Third-party identity trap:** EPR/group/religion/sect label equivalence cannot be inferred from matching surface strings.
24. **No person by research packet trap:** #1173 C4/C6 internally drawn candidate isn't yet a SOROK-issued independent adult/parent.

These are **proposed consumer-facing falsifiers**, not test executions reported by this document.

The source-level categories are *not* concept-level verdicts: a single original may contain separately adopted, conditional, downstream, held and rejected ideas, as the proposition court demonstrates. Each one has a proposition-level destination below; numbers of documents per category are not intellectual completeness. Every original record is indexed once. The companion three documents expand transfers, rejects and conditional optional methods. A `FILE_LOCATED` or `CHAT_HANDLE` grade is **not** a claim of close reading that source.

## XV-A. DIRECT ORIGINAL-PASSAGE PROPOSITION CONSERVATION COURT — SOURCE-STATIC, NOT FULL-ARCHIVE CLOSURE

**Why this exists:** the earlier one-source/one-disposition court below is bibliographically complete but can hide conflicts *within* an original source. This court treats each distinct intellectual operation, observation, refusal and proposed method separately, with an immutable original GitHub source **line range**, the original problem, the information or argument retained, whether it is mandatory/conditional/upstream/downstream/rejected/held, and what a real consumer loses if omitted. An origin can have **many different destinations**. The earlier 87-source ledger is NOT proof of 87 full intellectual extractions; the original source grades remain meaningful.

**Scope actually reopened for this court:** all 13 located N028 exploratory originals E020–E032 at their protected archive bytes, W48 architectural digest W036, and adopted R2 capsule A046. Selected sections were inspected in depth; document-level archival text, historical source authorizations and every other located-only source are not thereby universally revalidated. Older historical strong claims still defer to September R2 and live G4. Original E029 evidence grades are historical analytical distinctions, not an asserted current ABI or method grant.

**Disposition vocabulary:** `FF-CORE` = information the requested actor-independent source/field answer must preserve when applicable; `FF-COND` = claim-conditioned optional algorithm, representation or analysis, NOT universally needed; `UPSTREAM` = source/J/routing/permission semantic issuer remains original owner; `ISSUER` or `HOLD-SCI` = external structural/interpretive grant has not been proved; `DOWNSTREAM` = actor/world/temporal operation belongs to existing N9/V553–V558/SOROK owner; `RESEARCH` = method worth keeping but unselected; `REJECT-UNIVERSAL` = the original *universalization* is rejected without throwing away its legitimate question; `ADOPTED` = R2 minimum semantic law at adoption grain. Compound destinations are intentionally legitimate.

**No new work-order IDs:** these `E031.07`-style identifiers are citation anchors *within the original HOME*, not G4 capability identities, PROGRAMMA tasks, required artifacts or scientific issuances. A "first consumer" in the table is a proposed information demand, not proof a selected code path ran. The code HOME and actual owner's tests decide executed status. Quantities, denominators and uncertainty are governed by source/design permission, with complete-case observed ratios distinct from eligible-risk or weighted population inference.

**Four consumer-regime classes require different proof:** (i) original exact same-respondent repertoire (H051.02/.10/.11/.14); (ii) independently evidenced material/institutional power and action rights (H051.01/.04/.05/.15); (iii) historical source/measurement/transport (H051.06/.09/.12/.13); (iv) structurally issued downstream temporal, mediating and higher-order meaning (H051.07/.08/.16). A single successful direct sourcefree test of class (i) cannot accredit classes (ii)–(iv). No item here asserts current FFIF012D execution was repeated at this HOME SHA.

### Additional primary-source court — historical FFX algorithms, R2 owner ceilings, and the real post-R2 first-reader frontier

These 43 propositions are **source-level distinctions that change the constituent machine**, not merely references to documentation. The central correction is that three different things were previously blended: (1) historical FFX numeric calculations actually written in code; (2) optional future sociological MCA/GDA or homology estimators; and (3) current source-qualified/live person-world capability. These are not equivalent. Original R2 adoption's *actual positive ownership* includes direct typed structural-observatory relations and loss/negative receipts; an accepted prohibition on universal master carriers is not a prohibition on computational richness.

A secondary critical finding from the November 4 source-static first-reader court: **query hash and receipt echo of a generated-position binding do not imply that a V558 resolver applied the binding as an eligibility predicate**. This is a **targeted static candidate**, not an executed code regression or an automatic architecture failure. Current protected M4 source at `integration/pre-main-reconciliation-r1` was reread: `make_context_query` places `generated_binding_refs` into the query hash, and `resolve_v558` iterates stored items with identity/permission checks without an evident per-item CAN-generated-position applicability evaluation. The required two-position executable negative test remains unrun. A later owner may satisfy that predicate elsewhere; this HOME does not grant a replacement engine.

#### Reverse challenge against the positive machine

- **Exact joint and direct probability**: modern candidate #1247's RAW complete-case FF01/S04 differs from historical weighted FFX Core C. Use the same qualified source/estimand or state the difference; do not interchange denominators and certification.
- **Geometry as actual code**: historical Hamming/MDS functions and stability exist; empirical MCA/GDA and meaningful capital/field axes are *not implemented by that source*. If a named consumer demands a neighborhood, compare Hamming metric with exact direct joint; only then consider MCA if historical Hamming demonstrably fails its information need.
- **Structural power in use**: an independently qualified owner relation must affect a world-selected option or sanction. A PDF claim, a source quote or a V558 receipt that merely echoes a binding is not the proof.
- **Actor and temporal formation**: Phase A world, actor-generated position, Phase B requery, independent Other activity and later psychogenesis are downstream SOROK/V3 operations. Do not pull that code inside FF.
- **R2 authority**: typed absence, unknown scope, refusal, negative eligibility and model extrapolation are distinct. No architecture's attractive representation grants missing legal/survey/source/actor rights.

### Original V5 and APP: deeper early founding arguments under September R2 source/actor corrections

The earlier July/August vision is not dismissed merely because its nine-stage structure and phase numbers were later superseded. The **substantive arguments** were genuinely about material production of persons, source-observed social possibility, contested class/institution/identity relations, and uneliminable mediation. The following two original native Library documents were reopened directly in their full text and separated into 31 separate arguments. A source proposition can have multiple destinations: FF owes the actor-independent informational antecedent, a real institutional-source issuer may be held, and N9/SOROK owns the subsequent applied world or developmental act.

**Historic nomenclature correction:** August V5 names V555 generated household mediation and V556 living psychological flow. Modern PROGRAMMA V555 and V556 have established *actor-independent* relation-route and higher-order-route semantics respectively. **Do not resurrect the older stage labels as current runtime function ownership.** Preserve the historical scientific *question* and route it through modern V553–V558/N9/SOROK rather than force it into historical directories.

**The decisive early distinction:** `MATERIAL POWER/CONSTRAINT AT TIME AND PLACE` is not `A PARTICULAR PERSON'S INTERNALIZED PRESSURE-FLOW`. Field Forge's actor-independent object must answer who can act against whom, on what resources/terms, with what evidence grade or held source; plus what culture/repertoires are actually co-observed there. It must not fabricate what any particular organism notices, values, fears or ultimately refuses.

### The November 11 real first-consumer architecture — not a second Field Forge psyche

The two November 11 SOROK manuscripts were directly reopened as **original candidate primary sources**, not merely accepted from the earlier one-row-per-source synopsis. The following 35 separate propositions are selected for their bearing on the Field Forge supply requirement and its downstream seam. They do **not** accredit any November 11 design as implemented or replace present SOROK PROGRAMMA.

Their sharpest joint requirement is a truly *inhabited* world: employment rights, rent, obligations, actor-specific control over money, actual space/noise constraints, independent parental choices, a still-changing household and later tangible consequences. **FF must not turn the observation that parents hold certain opinions into a qualified tenancy law**, but where a lawful external source supplies that rule, the first world/actor reader must be able to use it. A selected material claim that merely appears in a narrative or source citation and never changes a real option fails the intended product.

The distinction between two derived views matters: a world owner may use an objectively admitted sanction to decide feasibility while an actor's *accessible* context excludes the reason until it is experienced. That is why passing a rich Field Forge packet wholesale into a prompt is neither the actor-independent structural computation nor the full historical-psychological organism.

### Constitutive source-field of *Others*: conditional D2 without invented contact — 48 additional direct propositions

New original SEAM sources H088 (November 16), H089 and H090 (November 17) were reopened after discovering their exact pointers inside H073 DCO R5. They address a question absent from a parent-only source sampling model: **the focal adult's respondent-coherent answer configuration does not define the complete differentiated society inhabited by parents, children and significant Others**. A second position D2 may have a different source-qualified distribution P(Y_joint|D2) and minority configurations, but that is not a source-measured social contact graph, family pairing or speaker.

**The necessary distinction is between potential Other source fields, actually available and institutionally mediated social alternatives, a specific world-selected Other/public claim, an actor's actual sensory/participatory access, and later acquired organization.** No step may be silently skipped. Yet D2 is not a mandatory per-event source: a real institutional rule or independently enacted public practice can affect the world without statistical selection of an Other. A cheap S04 conditional query may already supply D2 when needed. A selected actor/world sourcefree contact can be lawful without claiming a survey-estimated contact probability. Both paths need a named, rights-checked first reader.

**One minimal discriminating court** follows H090's sourcefree case 0→1→2: change only D2 distribution with no encounter (no focal change); admit a particular D2-conditioned or institution-conditioned world act (a concrete external change may occur); vary whether the child actually receives the cue (no automatic acquired motive or practice). An independent employer sanction with unchanged opinions is a material-right negative control. The present documents do not demonstrate an end-to-end tested D2→specific Other→later SOROK action, and do not turn #1011's narrow two-event pilot into a full society-formation gate.

### Later November 21–22 reciprocal consumer theories: no privileged social scenario

H084 R13 corrects earlier R5 interpretations by emphasizing that the *person’s* ongoing life and multiple currents must not be reduced to any labor-organizing, romantic or disciplinary test case. Its addition is relevant to FF precisely because **material permission/exercisable practical power is distinguishable from acquired skill, desire and personal initiative**. Independent Others and institutions set actual conditions, but do not program a response or require a dramatic scene.

H085 NEIGHBOR's current encounter theory is separately responsible for a real human visitor who begins on the same locally admitted economic footing yet has not thereby acquired Nadia's history, motives or intimate permission. H086's joined first-consumer theory exposes how a one-Other learning heuristic originally rejected two meals with father *and* Nadia present; a later R29 sourcefree fix allowed multiple co-present Others, while **not proving attention, causal attribution or true learned meaning**. Those code/test claims are treated as historic source reports at their own head, not re-executed here.

### 2025 ancestry — bounded memory-recovery exception, NOT Field Forge's design foundation

**Relevance ruling (2026-11-23, controlling user correction):** The original three PDFs D003–D005 were fully readable by direct PDF extraction at the current project attachment bytes. That repaired an extraction misconception. **It did not make the 2025 personality-generator framework constitutive Field Forge architecture.** Its detailed 49-row extraction had been overweighted, and is **withdrawn from the central constituent machine**. The PDFs remain retrievable in the historical source ledger by exact attachment filename, and this short archaeology note records the only relevant fates.

- **Potential forgotten insight:** an actual life and its culturally mediated contradictions must emerge through history, not a surface demographic label. This question is now better specified by the **May 11, June 25, August 10, August 30, September 2 and November actor/first-reader sources**.
- **Reject from FF:** weighted country/Hofstede/WVS averages→Enneagram type, country→individual psychic axis, invented confidence scores, harmony/obedience→health, and invented PI/DRTS/TT/RMT psychiatric thresholds. Neither current empirical source nor later first reader authorizes them.
- **Keep in SOROK, not FF:** developmental memory, acquired language and practice, conflicting identifications, caregiver activity and later reconstitution—only under actually generated life/events, not prompt-only chronology.
- **Evidence:** D003 (8-page), D004 (3-page), D005 (5-page) PDFs are *original historical artifacts* inspected by direct extraction, but proof of reading is not proof of implementation, scientific validity or relevance. If a specific *unresolved* present Field Forge question genuinely recurs in a pre-May text, reopen that exact page then; do not place entire 2025 system on equal footing with Field Forge's construction sequence.

### Immediate predecessor reconstruction — May–July 2026, the reason FIELD FORGE became necessary

**Relevance, not merely age:** these 28 original/provenance-grounded claims concern the *actual contradiction* inherited by N028: Rawlsing had an increasingly nuanced empirical source, answer, position and structural-pressure pipeline, yet could compute meaningful structural rows without delivering them as intelligible, legally usable relations to its next consumer. The resulting architecture could masquerade as pressure by counting permissions or demo keys. A fixed nine-strata hierarchy was a poor substitute for answering what differentiated social positions, conflicting repertoires, institutions and actual applicability make possible in a time/place.

The May 11 v2 chat is a **dated recovered-context lead**, not a retrieved complete manuscript. Its **actual original v2 pack contents** were recovered in the later ZIP as D098 and its implementation as D103, but the pack's May 11 authorship date was not independently verified. The June 20 original conversation D100 separately confirms fluid, context-dependent strata. The June 25 assessments are **original inspected historical reviews**, not claims of present runtime regressions. The July 6 earlier strata handoff is a lead; the direct August 10 V5 source and original August 29–31 N028 retain stronger authentication for its interpretation.

**What survives as an operational FF demand today:** actual complete-case/respondent/eligible topology; position≠attitude cloud; typed evidence atom/structural role with source/target/period/permissions; negative/refusal semantics; source-grain integrity through transforms; distinction between statistical association and externally issuable material force; and an exact first-reader operation whose output changes a downstream option. These are the *pre-N028 unresolved functional problems*, not an instruction to reinstate historical V552J/553 code or the old v3 directive as current programme.

## XVI. The remaining build-out described as *functions*, not new organs

**Controlling selection order:** `source question → G4 existing producer → exact semantics → first reader → witness of failure → smallest reuse/extension`.

The positive full-field vision reduces to these **functional sufficiency demands**; whether each needs code is a separate current-head investigation:

**Binding scope of A–H:** wherever applicable to a claim or discovery question, C/D/E/F must carry forward XI-A.1–2 and XII-A: the activity, resources, control and dependencies that reproduce or transform positions and institutions; directed institutional relevance, access and valuation through actual relations; and the reciprocal structural hypothesis → qualified empirical requery → revision, retention or hold circuit. These are necessary information distinctions, including for an intended but unimplemented recipient; optional representations and implementation selection remain subject to qualified incumbent comparison, source and owner authority, and claim-specific applicability. Neither a new module nor a universal execution prerequisite follows.

**A — Native source facts:** for all source-authorized family/wave/measurement contexts, preserve the exact source respondent, exposure/risk, J, weights and source-specific negative outcomes. Most belongs to C11/I03/I05; FF must consume—not copy—the result. C11 economics and rights remain live in parallel with SOURCEFREE.

**B — Source-grounded observed relational answers:** FF must be able to answer requested bounded conditioned *joint* distributions, genuinely unusual positive configurations, exceptions, source-local comparisons and nonadditive interactions, or refuse with precise observability/source constraints. Existing #1247 generic FF01/S04 has real code for substantial scope and is the first candidate to qualify.

**C — Claim-qualified social position:** use exact position-role authority and optional separately evidenced objective institution/asset relations, respecting source-local identity and disjoint attitude measurement ancestry. What an employer can actually enforce is N6/N9/SOROK-world authority, never manufactured by FF demographic inferences.

**D — Contextual alternatives, without ontology addiction:** optional local geometry, conditional profile, association motif, differential valuation, partial pooling or recurrence *only* where an existing direct conditional/joint query cannot supply the actual consumer's needed information. Preserve richer alternatives and negative states rather than collapsing to one label.

**E — Honest semantic tiers and material pathway:** descriptive source-observed sourcefree/model observations can proceed independently of unissued causal sociology. Positive strong relations require independently competent I04/material/N5 issuer. M1/M4/M5 and V553–V558 keep their own semantics/serving authority. N9 and SOROK enact the world.

**F — A functioning first-consumer loop:** a change in truly relevant actual source evidence changes a permitted conditional/possibility response; a separately changed material rule changes actual world options; actual actor practice/recruitment remains under SOROK. Each independent change is traceable and does not rewrite the other layer's evidence.

**G — Economical persistence and re-query:** choose direct producer query, bounded cache, source-owned index, sufficient-statistic carrier, or more storage only when actual repeated cold requests require it, without loss of source/negative/replay semantics.

**H — The ultimate purpose of Field Forge:** downstream can construct a materially *possible* historical person from a world where the represented pressures and alternatives are not narrative atmosphere but independently meaningful, conflicting and enforceable realities. The person remains contingent: historical life can surprise both the source frequencies and the model's explanatory vocabulary.

The **currently strongest sharply stated missing *proof*** is not "another Field Forge model". It is the selected end-to-end composition in which existing exact empirical/structural evidence reaches an actual substantive world first reader without fabricated authority, and then a separately actualized SOROK historical person. The user has explicitly rejected previous speculative broad FF construction plans; this HOME is **not** a rehabilitation of that backlog.

## XVI-A. Constitutive sufficiency — a positive answer to the six product questions, with the weakest real computational path

The inherited synthesis gives a sufficiently determinate *candidate* account of what Field Forge is for. It must not treat its subject matter as a pile of survey probabilities, nor presume that every differentiated social relation requires a new F5 program. The six controlling questions in PP26-13–19 and PP26-25 concern **relations between possible social positions, historically specific distributions, independently warranted structures and the availability of alternatives**. A material pressure can change actual eligibility, cost or consequence even if nobody's measured opinion changes. A change in observed collective repertoires can change a source-qualified possibility offer even if all material rules remain fixed. These are two positive results, not rival definitions of one scalar pressure.

### Three different compositions, not one fused distribution

1. **Observed comparison.** For a defined question, return an ordered set of source-qualified local distributions/support, e.g. exact `J(Y,D1,D2)` if these are attributes of the *same* respondent, or separate `J(Y,D1)` and `J(Y,D2)` when they describe two distinct social positions/populations. Preserve joint dependencies, typed uncertainty, relevant comparison baseline and actual scope. The conditional contrast describes what was observed, including nonadditive articulation; it cannot establish how the world enacted it. Upstream I03/S04 answers raw factual queries; an FF-specific claim adds an explicit *relation over those answers* only where that adds information needed by the actual reader.
2. **Independent structural alternatives.** For a specified arena, time and relevant positioned participant, distinguish a permitted route, an unavailable route, a contingent route, a materially costly one, an enforceable prohibition, and unresolved applicability. A particular resource can have contradictory effects across institutions; an employer's permission can coexist with the household's incompatible time requirement. Preserve the controlling entity, resource/obligation, rule, scope, counterpart, available alternatives and conflicting obligations when they are warranted. Two individually feasible uses of a finite shared resource need not be jointly feasible. Such conjunction and material constraint require the appropriate structural/world rule and owner, not a survey interaction coefficient. Do not sum two pressures simply because each has a numerical expression, or infer a route's probability from a group's response frequency.
3. **Changed-world comparison.** Compare two individually qualified contexts with one or more explicitly varied premises. Keep measured support unchanged if only an independently modeled rule changes. Keep material rules unchanged if only observed support changes. If both vary in an actual historical setting, record separately what changed, what is comparable, and which explanatory linkage remains a hypothesis. A neutral association contrast and a world-model intervention are different epistemic objects; an intervention inside a constructed world is not identification of an original-world causal effect. Later V553–V558/N9 and SOROK apply such actual model changes. FF's responsibility is the source-qualified actor-independent relational information, not authoring the resulting person.

The protected [nonparent value reader](https://github.com/maidiu/rawlsing/blob/827714e83743056939894a39736d72ea16bdc6dd/rawlsing_next/sourcefree_world/nonparent.py#L1-L34) actually demonstrates the narrow distinction in computation: the distinct nonauthor [review](https://github.com/maidiu/rawlsing/pull/1289#pullrequestreview-6033414824) reports exact-blob local execution of seven injected-boundary cases and seven passing assertions, including independent variation of civil sanction, empirical support, both, authority, costs and a shared-budget adversary. The earlier author execution assertion is retracted for lack of an established receipt; the independent reviewer report is separate evidence. The reported actual module returned separate structural routes and empirical balances; two individually affordable costs exceeding the same budget did not yield a joint-feasibility result. See code HOME II.J for exact scope. **It declines to emit a joint empirical/structural route probability.** That is an honest and useful boundary, not evidence that general interfield material interaction is already implemented. There is no obligation to define a universal numerical pressure or universal causal model to make this separation operational.

### Whole-survey correspondence and differentiated surrounding Others

An adult entrance that corresponds to an entire survey is **not** a single dense Cartesian distribution over every question. Nor is it arbitrary independent completion of every question that happened to be listed in a codebook. The exact response topology may include disjoint forms, repeated/multiselect events, unasked items, unresolved route, and incomplete same-unit joint. Whole-survey correspondence is a requirement to preserve and use the whole available governed evidence envelope, not an instruction to pretend there was one common risk for all axes. Retain the actually queried context and joint ancestry, exact accepted prior choices and constraints, source-local support/refusal, and explicit model or unresolved completion when exact conditional support ends. If a succession of conditionals is to represent the same joint, each conditional must genuinely be conditional on the selected prefix under a consistent law; changing risk/denominator/measurement regime without an issued bridge changes the claim. Neither pairwise factorization nor stitching noncomparable partial joints meets that requirement. If two disjoint forms measure A and B conditional on shared D, even exact P(A|D) and P(B|D) do not identify P(A,B|D). A fictional adult may receive an explicitly modeled completion with justified bounds or uncertainty, not an observed same-person joint. Eventual calibration must compare generated adults' observable projections to the applicable instrument/risk/estimand, not treat unmeasured answers as confirmed. Existing progressive/recursive queries and bounded sourcefree C4/C5/C6 are the first incumbents, not evidence a full-survey adult already exists. C4 samples one complete finite cell; FF01's full-resolution and 16,384-cell ceiling is material to this recipient. The next exact selected source query must establish whether existing sparse/re-query or conditioned subproblem paths suffice; source-use/weight/semantics stay upstream and actual person selection SOROK-owned.

For surrounding Others, [H089](https://github.com/maidiu/rawlsing/blob/4d1a6195daca54b4dd63491f55c1eff73907f534/SEAM/28_POST_SEAM26_SOCIAL_FIELD_RECRUITMENT_AND_ANVIL_LESSONS_20261117_R1.md) and [H090](https://github.com/maidiu/rawlsing/blob/4d1a6195daca54b4dd63491f55c1eff73907f534/SEAM/29_UM_PYAT_TO_UM_SOROK_CROSS_FIELD_RECRUITMENT_FIRST_READER_CHALLENGE_20261117_R1.md) demand two competing legal entrances. A relation-first question can ask whether different surrounding positions have different observed repertoires while the focal conditional is fixed. The distribution informs candidate possibilities for independently realized Others, not actual contact, assortative selection, influence or mutual belief. An institution/practice-first question may instead be answerable from an independently warranted norm, rule, activity or opportunity without any D2 sampled opinion. The chosen world's actual recipient decides which route adds a substantive difference; one cannot force every person to meet a particular sample-conditioned Other. A source distribution over D2 is not a joint distribution of the attitudes of two independently situated people, and not a social network. If no encounter occurred, a changed surrounding distribution alone cannot imply focal psychic change. If actual institutional rules changed, available alternatives can change before anyone acquires a new disposition. Preserve both routes, not a universal other-field generator.

**Revised author sufficiency position (B1–B4 candidate, not independent acceptance):** the existing conceptual corpus supplies a meaningful answer-form for all six product questions, including differentiated other-position contexts, contradictory joint and material constraints, directional value/power, temporal scope, negative evidence and changed conditions. Its strongest residuals are now **specific empirical/issuer/first-consumer and causal-mechanism questions**, not an evidenced need for a new universal FF organ. No *unconditional* additional THEORYSEARCH or implementation is selected while #1290's horizontal ownership is unreceived. This does not extinguish conceptual needs or autonomous discovery questions. B1–B3 add reproduction, dynamic relevance and reciprocal inquiry as indispensable information demands; their implementation allocation remains subject to existing owners and actual evidence. A conceptual or discovery question may remain unresolved independently of any currently functioning recipient. A claim of missing implementation or selection of new engineering requires comparison with the qualified incumbent and the information needs of an intended reader or discovery operation; executed first-reader failure is evidence where such a reader exists, not a prerequisite to recognizing the need. At this author cut the positive conceptual HOME is fit for independent examination; there is no evidentiary basis for claiming definitive FF-only residual closure, strong sociology issuance, original-source science or actor execution. Missing May11/July6 transcript provenance is held but does not by itself veto this candidate account.

It brings the acquired estate and executed R6 readers into the full intended machine. Earlier distinctions remain binding, particularly XI-A reproduction, relational relevance, reciprocal inquiry and separate semantic tiers. Actual progressive/cohort-aware native FF comparison remains open; do not build new joint math. |

No fixed target or exhaustive causal sociology. |

### The constructive circuit (logical dataflow, not a mandatory serial ABI)

1. A request arrives from a particular life context, or from an empirical/structural investigation. It identifies the actor-independent question: position, other-position repertoire, independently relevant relation, or altered institutional alternative. Demand can emerge before a current receiver exists; do not confuse conceptual necessity and engineering readiness.
2. Retrieve native survey evidence via I03/S04/I05 and independent external context via appropriate native reader. Each keeps own release, period, unit, risk, keys, source and interpretation. Use only relevant sources; no global joined vector. Existing exact source relation resolver governs true cross-source composition. For the already measured adult configuration, do not resample it from a country margin.
3. FIELD FORGE constructs a scoped relational answer: the compared distributions and support, position/correspondence status, independent material/social relation, relevant control/recognition/dependence, actual differences and missing constraints, qualified competing explanations. It can return a neutral contrast, ask a next discriminator of existing I05 or source owner, or hold only a stronger claim. This is the FF transformation; upstream numerical products alone do not interpret the difference, and FF does not issue a causal field without evidence.
4. Post-FF world/possibility owners admit particular context/role/rule and retain the separate empirical product. A world may use an explicit modeled assumption when a source doesn't determine a detail. Existing N9/N10W and material owners apply actual resource/eligibility/recognition rules. No group frequency as probability of actor action, no fake J04 binding, no route probability without evidence. A changed independently modeled constraint must produce a value change at a substantive actual reader.
5. In the particular world, two paths remain available: (a) D2-conditioned potential Other → independently particularized persistent Other → actual action/expression; (b) institution/practice/public event → actual material or symbolic consequence without a sampled Other. Both require actual reach/access, not automatic exposure. Preserve unperceived material changes and strategic public/private divergence.
6. The existing SOROK person machinery gives the lived event meaning through its own bodily needs, history and recruitment. An LLM can propose situated interpretation, a novel acquired organization or no durable acquisition; admitted operation must produce actual state change, not just a narrative. Subsequent response/tip → world consequence → psychic reconstitution → future recruitment. Ordinary life can be compressed without dropping consequential needs, activity, relationships or resource renewal. Actor, independent Other and world remain historically continuous.
7. Actual actor/world changes can produce new relevant questions of FF, but never edit source survey facts. A difference in source candidate Other distributions alone changes possible future offers, not already existing people. A changed material condition can affect the developing organism without that organism knowing why.

### The two historical entrances in this completed machine

**Parents:** Source-conditioned whole-survey-correspondent fictional adults are generated through coherent progressive evidence and materialist-psychological abduction. Measured answer repertoire constrains possible expression under that instrument, not a unique hidden psyche or history. Different coherent historical formations can produce the same response. Existing SEAM compressed parent history and SOROK owners select the actual parents/partner/conception and operative world; no observed survey marriage or copied real respondent. Preserve source-unknown rather than force a fake joint. FF supplies independently structured living conditions and surrounding possibilities; actual parent history is not an FF output.

**Nadia:** an immature need-bearing organism develops through actual evolving household, work, care, school, kin, peer and other institutions. Parents' histories and public practices become formative via their actual acts and her access, not transfer of their survey values. Other-first and institution-first routes distinguish possible contact, actual exposure, appropriation and later use. No predetermined adult destination. Adult surveys can inform adult Others; fictional youth/peer culture can be constructed within the setting without claiming measured youth attitudes. The same continuing SOROK psychology operates after both entrances.

### Concrete construction convergence, not another exploratory campaign

The first genuinely FF-specific increment is not another source inventory. Take a useful selected comparison using the R6 independent native value product and the existing I03/S04 conditional product, once appropriately qualified. Produce an actor-independent *relation over both* at the actual required grain (for example differing independently observed group executive status under matched group repertoire, retaining denominators and crosswalk ceiling). If the actual crosswalk is not available, return separate contextual and empirical results, and a precise unresolved relation; do not halt unrelated construction. Use the existing query outputs rather than make a replacement EmpiricalFact. ### VI-B continuation — R11: entitlement is not one disposition

The two scarce-job priority norms can travel together, separate, reverse their relation across reported employment positions, or be unavailable in a given risk. The first candidate concept is **selective entitlement under narrated scarcity**, not a person type. A rival is a unified exclusion norm relatively independent of employment position. A mixed motif, conditioned on employment and then used to ask the reverse question, can differentiate the descriptive alternatives; it cannot settle why anyone endorsed a statement or whether an employer actually acts that way. Nor can a questionnaire's scarcity premise establish actual scarcity. An institution may enforce one boundary while refusing another, regardless of the majority's answers. Independently constituted resources, wage dependence and alternative provision change the stakes and practicable refusal of that norm. The same empirical repertoire can inhabit different arrangements of control.

The construction now has a specific iterative operation in the existing relational reader, taking actual S04/FF01 sourcefree product, independent material context, and provisional concept/rival/question, then returning the incumbent reverse inquiry and revised possibility. This is one concrete HULCH connection, not an ontology, estimator or automatic social interpretation. The LLM interface is callback-based; model-authored proposals are genuine candidates, but no live model transport has been executed. The retained source question is WVS W7 Q33/Q34/Q279 (see 00 R11); its actual native coadministration and original-source analysis remain held. The selected sourcefree fixture is explicitly synthetic. A downstream historical possibility would distinguish the two public priority norms, actual rule application, and alternative access to subsistence; the particular person, contact, action, and acquisition are not inferred. See 01 II.O and 04 R11.
